Implement the following plan:

# Plan: Add Copilot E2E Test Support

## Context

The copilot agent is implemented and can be enabled via `entire enable --agent github-copilot`, but the e2e test suite (`cmd/entire/cli/e2e_test/`) has no `CopilotRunner`. Running `E2E_AGENT=github-copilot` falls through to `unavailableRunner`. This plan adds copilot support so existing e2e scenarios work with `E2E_AGENT=github-copilot`.

The Copilot CLI (`copilot` v0.0.411) is installed locally and supports non-interactive mode via `-p "prompt"` with `--model` and `--allow-all-tools` flags — very similar to Claude Code.

## Changes

### 1. Add `CopilotRunner` to `e2e_test/agent_runner.go`

Add constant and runner:

```go
const AgentNameCopilot = "github-copilot"
```

**`CopilotRunner` struct**: `Model string`, `Timeout time.Duration`

**`NewCopilotRunner(config)`**:
- Model: `config.Model` || `E2E_COPILOT_MODEL` || `"claude-haiku-4.5"` (cheapest)
- Timeout: `config.Timeout` || `E2E_TIMEOUT` || `2m`

**`IsAvailable()`**:
- `exec.LookPath("copilot")`
- `copilot --version` with 10s timeout

**`RunPromptWithTools(ctx, workDir, prompt, tools)`**:
- Command: `copilot --model <model> -p "<prompt>" --allow-all-tools`
- If `tools` provided: add `--available-tools <tools...>`
- Same stdout/stderr/exitcode capture pattern as `ClaudeCodeRunner`

**`RunPrompt()`**: delegates to `RunPromptWithTools` with `nil` tools

**Register in `NewAgentRunner` switch**:
```go
case AgentNameCopilot:
    return NewCopilotRunner(config)
```

### 2. Add copilot session dir env var to `e2e_test/testenv.go`

The copilot agent's `GetSessionDir()` checks `ENTIRE_TEST_COPILOT_SESSION_DIR`. Without it, hooks try to read `~/.copilot/session-state/` which won't have test session data.

In **3 methods** that set hook env vars — `GitCommitWithShadowHooks`, `GitCommitStagedWithShadowHooks`, `GitCommitWithTrailerRemoved` — add:

```go
"ENTIRE_TEST_COPILOT_SESSION_DIR=" + filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".copilot-sessions"),
```

This mirrors the existing `ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR` and `ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR` patterns.

## Files

| File | Change |
|---|---|
| `cmd/entire/cli/e2e_test/agent_runner.go` | Add `AgentNameCopilot`, `CopilotRunner`, register in factory |
| `cmd/entire/cli/e2e_test/testenv.go` | Add `ENTIRE_TEST_COPILOT_SESSION_DIR` to 3 hook env blocks |

## Verification

```bash
# Build and run a single e2e test with copilot
E2E_AGENT=github-copilot go test -tags=e2e -run TestE2E_BasicWorkflow ./cmd/entire/cli/e2e_test/...

# If copilot not authenticated, tests should skip gracefully (exit 0)
```

    16→// AgentNameClaudeCode is the name for Claude Code agent.

    19→// AgentNameGemini is the name for Gemini CLI agent.

    21→
    22→// AgentRunner abstracts invoking a coding agent for e2e tests.
    23→// This follows the multi-agent pattern from cmd/entire/cli/agent/agent.go.
    24→type AgentRunner interface {
    25→	// Name returns the agent name (e.g., "claude-code", "gemini-cli")
    26→	Name() string
    27→
    28→	// IsAvailable checks if the agent CLI is installed and authenticated
    29→	IsAvailable() (bool, error)
    30→
    31→	// RunPrompt executes a prompt and returns the result
    32→	RunPrompt(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string) (*AgentResult, error)
    33→
    34→	// RunPromptWithTools executes with specific allowed tools
    35→	RunPromptWithTools(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string, tools []string) (*AgentResult, error)
    36→}
    37→
    38→// AgentResult holds the result of an agent invocation.
    39→type AgentResult struct {
    40→	Stdout   string
    41→	Stderr   string
    42→	ExitCode int
    43→	Duration time.Duration
    44→}
    45→
    46→// AgentRunnerConfig holds configuration for agent runners.
    47→type AgentRunnerConfig struct {
    48→	Model   string        // Model to use (e.g., "haiku" for Claude)
    49→	Timeout time.Duration // Timeout per prompt
    50→}
    51→
    52→// NewAgentRunner creates an agent runner based on the agent name.
    53→//
    54→//nolint:ireturn // factory pattern intentionally returns interface
    55→func NewAgentRunner(name string, config AgentRunnerConfig) AgentRunner {
    56→	switch name {
    57→	case AgentNameClaudeCode:
    58→		return NewClaudeCodeRunner(config)
    59→	case AgentNameGemini:
    60→		return NewGeminiCLIRunner(config)
    61→	default:
    62→		// Return a runner that reports as unavailable
    63→		return &unavailableRunner{name: name}
    64→	}
    65→}
    66→
    67→// unavailableRunner is returned for unknown agent names.
    68→type unavailableRunner struct {
    69→	name string
    70→}
    71→
    72→func (r *unavailableRunner) Name() string { return r.name }
    73→

    76→}
    77→

    85→
    86→// ClaudeCodeRunner implements AgentRunner for Claude Code CLI.
    87→type ClaudeCodeRunner struct {
    88→	Model        string
    89→	Timeout      time.Duration
    90→	AllowedTools []string
    91→}
    92→
    93→// NewClaudeCodeRunner creates a new Claude Code runner with the given config.
    94→func NewClaudeCodeRunner(config AgentRunnerConfig) *ClaudeCodeRunner {
    95→	model := config.Model
    96→	if model == "" {
    97→		model = os.Getenv("E2E_CLAUDE_MODEL")
    98→		if model == "" {
    99→			model = "haiku"
   100→		}
   101→	}
   102→
   103→	timeout := config.Timeout
   104→	if timeout == 0 {
   105→		if envTimeout := os.Getenv("E2E_TIMEOUT"); envTimeout != "" {
   106→			if parsed, err := time.ParseDuration(envTimeout); err == nil {
   107→				timeout = parsed
   108→			}
   109→		}
   110→		if timeout == 0 {
   111→			timeout = 2 * time.Minute
   112→		}
   113→	}
   114→
   115→	return &ClaudeCodeRunner{
   116→		Model:        model,
   117→		Timeout:      timeout,
   118→		AllowedTools: []string{"Edit", "Read", "Write", "Bash", "Glob", "Grep"},
   119→	}
   120→}
   121→
   122→func (r *ClaudeCodeRunner) Name() string {
   123→	return AgentNameClaudeCode
   124→}
   125→
   126→// IsAvailable checks if Claude CLI is installed and responds to --version.
   127→// Note: This does NOT verify authentication status. Claude Code uses OAuth
   128→// authentication (via `claude login`), not ANTHROPIC_API_KEY. If the CLI is
   129→// installed but not logged in, tests will fail at RunPrompt time.
   130→func (r *ClaudeCodeRunner) IsAvailable() (bool, error) {
   131→	// Check if claude CLI is in PATH
   132→	if _, err := exec.LookPath("claude"); err != nil {
   133→		return false, fmt.Errorf("claude CLI not found in PATH: %w", err)
   134→	}
   135→
   136→	// Check if claude is working (--version doesn't require auth)
   137→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 10*time.Second)
   138→	defer cancel()
   139→
   140→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "claude", "--version")
   141→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   142→		return false, fmt.Errorf("claude CLI not working: %w", err)
   143→	}
   144→
   145→	return true, nil
   146→}
   147→
   148→func (r *ClaudeCodeRunner) RunPrompt(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string) (*AgentResult, error) {
   149→	return r.RunPromptWithTools(ctx, workDir, prompt, r.AllowedTools)
   150→}
   151→
   152→func (r *ClaudeCodeRunner) RunPromptWithTools(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string, tools []string) (*AgentResult, error) {
   153→	// Build command: claude --model <model> -p "<prompt>" --allowedTools <tools>
   154→	args := []string{
   155→		"--model", r.Model,
   156→		"-p", prompt,
   157→	}
   158→
   159→	if len(tools) > 0 {
   160→		// Claude CLI expects each tool as a separate argument after --allowedTools
   161→		// e.g., --allowedTools Edit Read Bash (not --allowedTools "Edit,Read,Bash")
   162→		args = append(args, "--allowedTools")
   163→		args = append(args, tools...)
   164→	}
   165→
   166→	// Create context with timeout
   167→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, r.Timeout)
   168→	defer cancel()
   169→
   170→	//nolint:gosec // args are constructed from trusted config, not user input
   171→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "claude", args...)
   172→	cmd.Dir = workDir
   173→
   174→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   175→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   176→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   177→
   178→	start := time.Now()
   179→	err := cmd.Run()
   180→	duration := time.Since(start)
   181→
   182→	result := &AgentResult{
   183→		Stdout:   stdout.String(),
   184→		Stderr:   stderr.String(),
   185→		Duration: duration,
   186→	}
   187→
   188→	if err != nil {
   189→		exitErr := &exec.ExitError{}
   190→		if errors.As(err, &exitErr) {
   191→			result.ExitCode = exitErr.ExitCode()
   192→		} else {
   193→			result.ExitCode = -1
   194→		}
   195→		//nolint:wrapcheck // error is from exec.Run, caller can check ExitCode in result
   196→		return result, err
   197→	}
   198→
   199→	result.ExitCode = 0
   200→	return result, nil
   201→}
   202→
   203→// GeminiCLIRunner implements AgentRunner for Gemini CLI.
   204→// See: https://github.com/google-gemini/gemini-cli
   205→type GeminiCLIRunner struct {
   206→	Model   string
   207→	Timeout time.Duration
   208→}
   209→
   210→// NewGeminiCLIRunner creates a new Gemini CLI runner with the given config.
   211→func NewGeminiCLIRunner(config AgentRunnerConfig) *GeminiCLIRunner {
   212→	model := config.Model
   213→	if model == "" {
   214→		model = os.Getenv("E2E_GEMINI_MODEL")
   215→		// Default to gemini-2.5-flash if not specified
   216→		if model == "" {
   217→			model = "gemini-2.5-flash"
   218→		}
   219→	}
   220→
   221→	timeout := config.Timeout
   222→	if timeout == 0 {
   223→		if envTimeout := os.Getenv("E2E_TIMEOUT"); envTimeout != "" {
   224→			if parsed, err := time.ParseDuration(envTimeout); err == nil {
   225→				timeout = parsed
   226→			}
   227→		}
   228→		if timeout == 0 {
   229→			timeout = 2 * time.Minute
   230→		}
   231→	}
   232→
   233→	return &GeminiCLIRunner{
   234→		Model:   model,
   235→		Timeout: timeout,
   236→	}
   237→}
   238→
   239→func (r *GeminiCLIRunner) Name() string {
   240→	return AgentNameGemini
   241→}
   242→
   243→// IsAvailable checks if Gemini CLI is installed and authenticated.
   244→func (r *GeminiCLIRunner) IsAvailable() (bool, error) {
   245→	// Check if gemini CLI is in PATH
   246→	if _, err := exec.LookPath("gemini"); err != nil {
   247→		return false, fmt.Errorf("gemini CLI not found in PATH: %w", err)
   248→	}
   249→
   250→	// Check if gemini is working (--version doesn't require auth)
   251→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 10*time.Second)
   252→	defer cancel()
   253→
   254→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "gemini", "--version")
   255→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   256→		return false, fmt.Errorf("gemini CLI not working: %w", err)
   257→	}
   258→
   259→	return true, nil
   260→}
   261→
   262→func (r *GeminiCLIRunner) RunPrompt(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string) (*AgentResult, error) {
   263→	return r.RunPromptWithTools(ctx, workDir, prompt, nil)
   264→}
   265→
   266→func (r *GeminiCLIRunner) RunPromptWithTools(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string, tools []string) (*AgentResult, error) {
   267→	// Build command: gemini -m <model> -p "<prompt>" --approval-mode auto_edit --allowed-tools <tools>
   268→	// --approval-mode auto_edit: auto-approves edit tools (write_file, replace) while prompting for others
   269→	// --allowed-tools: comma-separated list of tools that bypass confirmation (e.g., git commands)
   270→	args := []string{
   271→		"-m", r.Model,
   272→		"-p", prompt,
   273→		"--approval-mode", "auto_edit",
   274→	}
   275→
   276→	// Add default git tools that should be allowed without confirmation
   277→	defaultAllowedTools := []string{
   278→		"ShellTool(git status)",
   279→		"ShellTool(git add)",
   280→		"ShellTool(git commit)",
   281→		"ShellTool(git diff)",
   282→		"ShellTool(git log)",
   283→	}
   284→
   285→	// Merge with any additional tools passed in
   286→	allTools := append(defaultAllowedTools, tools...)
   287→	if len(allTools) > 0 {
   288→		args = append(args, "--allowed-tools", strings.Join(allTools, ","))
   289→	}
   290→
   291→	// Create context with timeout
   292→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, r.Timeout)
   293→	defer cancel()
   294→
   295→	//nolint:gosec // args are constructed from trusted config, not user input
   296→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "gemini", args...)
   297→	cmd.Dir = workDir
   298→
   299→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   300→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   301→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   302→
   303→	start := time.Now()
   304→	err := cmd.Run()
   305→	duration := time.Since(start)
   306→
   307→	result := &AgentResult{
   308→		Stdout:   stdout.String(),
   309→		Stderr:   stderr.String(),
   310→		Duration: duration,
   311→	}
   312→
   313→	if err != nil {
   314→		exitErr := &exec.ExitError{}
   315→		if errors.As(err, &exitErr) {
   316→			result.ExitCode = exitErr.ExitCode()
   317→		} else {
   318→			result.ExitCode = -1
   319→		}
   320→		//nolint:wrapcheck // error is from exec.Run, caller can check ExitCode in result
   321→		return result, err
   322→	}
   323→
   324→	result.ExitCode = 0
   325→	return result, nil
   326→}
   327→

     1→//go:build e2e
     2→
     3→package e2e
     4→
     5→import (
     6→	"context"
     7→	"crypto/sha256"
     8→	"encoding/hex"
     9→	"encoding/json"
    10→	"errors"
    11→	"os"
    12→	"os/exec"
    13→	"path/filepath"
    14→	"strings"
    15→	"testing"
    16→	"time"
    17→
    18→	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5"
    19→	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing"
    20→	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/format/config"
    21→	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/object"
    22→)
    23→
    24→// defaultAgent holds the agent to test with, determined in TestMain.
    25→var defaultAgent string
    26→
    27→// testBinaryPath holds the path to the CLI binary built once in TestMain.
    28→// All tests share this binary to avoid repeated builds.
    29→var testBinaryPath string
    30→
    31→// TestEnv manages an isolated test environment for E2E tests with real agent calls.
    32→type TestEnv struct {
    33→	T       *testing.T
    34→	RepoDir string
    35→	Agent   AgentRunner
    36→}
    37→
    38→// getTestBinary returns the path to the shared test binary.
    39→// It panics if TestMain hasn't run (testBinaryPath is empty).
    40→func getTestBinary() string {
    41→	if testBinaryPath == "" {
    42→		panic("testBinaryPath not set - TestMain must run before tests")
    43→	}
    44→	return testBinaryPath
    45→}
    46→
    47→// NewTestEnv creates a new isolated E2E test environment.
    48→func NewTestEnv(t *testing.T) *TestEnv {
    49→	t.Helper()
    50→
    51→	// Resolve symlinks on macOS where /var -> /private/var
    52→	repoDir := t.TempDir()
    53→	if resolved, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(repoDir); err == nil {
    54→		repoDir = resolved
    55→	}
    56→
    57→	// Create agent runner
    58→	agent := NewAgentRunner(defaultAgent, AgentRunnerConfig{})
    59→
    60→	return &TestEnv{
    61→		T:       t,
    62→		RepoDir: repoDir,
    63→		Agent:   agent,
    64→	}
    65→}
    66→
    67→// NewFeatureBranchEnv creates an E2E test environment ready for testing.
    68→// It initializes the repo, creates an initial commit on main,
    69→// checks out a feature branch, and sets up agent hooks.
    70→func NewFeatureBranchEnv(t *testing.T, strategyName string) *TestEnv {
    71→	t.Helper()
    72→
    73→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
    74→	env.InitRepo()
    75→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test Repository\n\nThis is a test repository for E2E testing.\n")
    76→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
    77→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
    78→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/e2e-test")
    79→
    80→	// Use `entire enable` to set up everything (hooks, settings, etc.)
    81→	// This sets up .entire/settings.json and .claude/settings.json with hooks
    82→	env.RunEntireEnable(strategyName)
    83→
    84→	// Commit all files created by `entire enable` so they survive git stash -u operations.
    85→	// Without this, stash operations would stash away the hooks config and entire settings,
    86→	// causing hooks to not fire for subsequent prompts and stash pop conflicts.
    87→	// Using "git add ." is safe in test repos since we control all content.
    88→	env.GitAddAll()
    89→	env.GitCommit("Add entire and agent config")
    90→
    91→	return env
    92→}
    93→
    94→// RunEntireEnable runs `entire enable` to set up the project with hooks.
    95→// Uses the configured defaultAgent (from E2E_AGENT env var or "claude-code").
    96→func (env *TestEnv) RunEntireEnable(strategyName string) {
    97→	env.T.Helper()
    98→
    99→	args := []string{
   100→		"enable",
   101→		"--agent", defaultAgent,
   102→		"--strategy", strategyName,
   103→		"--telemetry=false",
   104→		"--force", // Force reinstall hooks in case they exist
   105→	}
   106→
   107→	//nolint:gosec,noctx // test code, args are static
   108→	cmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), args...)
   109→	cmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   110→
   111→	output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()
   112→	if err != nil {
   113→		env.T.Fatalf("entire enable failed: %v\nOutput: %s", err, output)
   114→	}
   115→	env.T.Logf("entire enable output: %s", output)
   116→}
   117→
   118→// InitRepo initializes a git repository in the test environment.
   119→func (env *TestEnv) InitRepo() {
   120→	env.T.Helper()
   121→
   122→	repo, err := git.PlainInit(env.RepoDir, false)
   123→	if err != nil {
   124→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to init git repo: %v", err)
   125→	}
   126→
   127→	// Configure git user for commits
   128→	cfg, err := repo.Config()
   129→	if err != nil {
   130→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get repo config: %v", err)
   131→	}
   132→	cfg.User.Name = "E2E Test User"
   133→	cfg.User.Email = "e2e-test@example.com"
   134→
   135→	// Disable GPG signing for test commits
   136→	if cfg.Raw == nil {
   137→		cfg.Raw = config.New()
   138→	}
   139→	cfg.Raw.Section("commit").SetOption("gpgsign", "false")
   140→
   141→	if err := repo.SetConfig(cfg); err != nil {
   142→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to set repo config: %v", err)
   143→	}
   144→}
   145→
   146→// WriteFile creates a file with the given content in the test repo.
   147→func (env *TestEnv) WriteFile(path, content string) {
   148→	env.T.Helper()
   149→
   150→	fullPath := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, path)
   151→
   152→	// Create parent directories
   153→	dir := filepath.Dir(fullPath)
   154→	//nolint:gosec // test code, permissions are intentionally standard
   155→	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o755); err != nil {
   156→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to create directory %s: %v", dir, err)
   157→	}
   158→
   159→	//nolint:gosec // test code, permissions are intentionally standard
   160→	if err := os.WriteFile(fullPath, []byte(content), 0o644); err != nil {
   161→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to write file %s: %v", path, err)
   162→	}
   163→}
   164→
   165→// ReadFile reads a file from the test repo.
   166→func (env *TestEnv) ReadFile(path string) string {
   167→	env.T.Helper()
   168→
   169→	fullPath := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, path)
   170→	//nolint:gosec // test code, path is from test setup
   171→	data, err := os.ReadFile(fullPath)
   172→	if err != nil {
   173→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to read file %s: %v", path, err)
   174→	}
   175→	return string(data)
   176→}
   177→
   178→// TryReadFile reads a file from the test repo, returning empty string if not found.
   179→func (env *TestEnv) TryReadFile(path string) string {
   180→	env.T.Helper()
   181→
   182→	fullPath := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, path)

   259→// GitCommitWithShadowHooks stages and commits files, running the prepare-commit-msg
   260→// and post-commit hooks like a real workflow.
   261→func (env *TestEnv) GitCommitWithShadowHooks(message string, files ...string) {
   262→	env.T.Helper()
   263→
   264→	// Stage files using go-git
   265→	for _, file := range files {
   266→		env.GitAdd(file)
   267→	}
   268→
   269→	// Create a temp file for the commit message
   270→	msgFile := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".git", "COMMIT_EDITMSG")
   271→	//nolint:gosec // test code, permissions are intentionally standard
   272→	if err := os.WriteFile(msgFile, []byte(message), 0o644); err != nil {
   273→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to write commit message file: %v", err)
   274→	}
   275→
   276→	// Run prepare-commit-msg hook
   277→	//nolint:gosec,noctx // test code, args are from trusted test setup, no context needed
   278→	prepCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "prepare-commit-msg", msgFile, "message")
   279→	prepCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   280→	prepCmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
   281→		"ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1",
   282→		"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".claude"),
   283→		"ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".gemini"),
   284→	)
   285→	if output, err := prepCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
   286→		env.T.Logf("prepare-commit-msg output: %s", output)
   287→	}
   288→
   289→	// Read the modified message
   290→	//nolint:gosec // test code, path is from test setup
   291→	modifiedMsg, err := os.ReadFile(msgFile)
   292→	if err != nil {
   293→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to read modified commit message: %v", err)
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	// Create the commit using go-git with the modified message
   297→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   298→	if err != nil {
   299→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   300→	}
   301→
   302→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   303→	if err != nil {
   304→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get worktree: %v", err)
   305→	}
   306→
   307→	_, err = worktree.Commit(string(modifiedMsg), &git.CommitOptions{
   308→		Author: &object.Signature{
   309→			Name:  "E2E Test User",
   310→			Email: "e2e-test@example.com",
   311→			When:  time.Now(),
   312→		},
   313→	})
   314→	if err != nil {
   315→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to commit: %v", err)
   316→	}
   317→
   318→	// Run post-commit hook
   319→	//nolint:gosec,noctx // test code, args are from trusted test setup, no context needed
   320→	postCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "post-commit")
   321→	postCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   322→	postCmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
   323→		"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".claude"),
   324→		"ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".gemini"),
   325→	)
   326→	if output, err := postCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {

   412→// GitCommitWithTrailerRemoved stages and commits files, simulating what happens when
   413→// a user removes the Entire-Checkpoint trailer during commit message editing.
   414→// This tests the opt-out behavior where removing the trailer skips condensation.
   415→func (env *TestEnv) GitCommitWithTrailerRemoved(message string, files ...string) {
   416→	env.T.Helper()
   417→
   418→	// Stage files using go-git
   419→	for _, file := range files {
   420→		env.GitAdd(file)
   421→	}
   422→
   423→	// Create a temp file for the commit message
   424→	msgFile := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".git", "COMMIT_EDITMSG")
   425→	//nolint:gosec // test code, permissions are intentionally standard
   426→	if err := os.WriteFile(msgFile, []byte(message), 0o644); err != nil {
   427→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to write commit message file: %v", err)
   428→	}
   429→
   430→	// Run prepare-commit-msg hook
   431→	//nolint:gosec,noctx // test code, args are from trusted test setup, no context needed
   432→	prepCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "prepare-commit-msg", msgFile)
   433→	prepCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   434→	prepCmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
   435→		"ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1",
   436→		"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".claude"),
   437→		"ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".gemini"),
   438→	)
   439→	if output, err := prepCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
   440→		env.T.Logf("prepare-commit-msg output: %s", output)
   441→	}
   442→
   443→	// Read the modified message (with trailer added by hook)
   444→	//nolint:gosec // test code, path is from test setup
   445→	modifiedMsg, err := os.ReadFile(msgFile)
   446→	if err != nil {
   447→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to read modified commit message: %v", err)
   448→	}
   449→
   450→	// REMOVE the Entire-Checkpoint trailer (simulating user editing the message)
   451→	lines := strings.Split(string(modifiedMsg), "\n")
   452→	var cleanedLines []string
   453→	for _, line := range lines {
   454→		if strings.HasPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:") {
   455→			continue
   456→		}
   457→		if strings.Contains(line, "Remove the Entire-Checkpoint trailer") {
   458→			continue
   459→		}
   460→		if strings.Contains(line, "trailer will be added to your next commit") {
   461→			continue
   462→		}
   463→		cleanedLines = append(cleanedLines, line)
   464→	}
   465→	cleanedMsg := strings.TrimRight(strings.Join(cleanedLines, "\n"), "\n") + "\n"
   466→
   467→	// Create the commit using go-git with the cleaned message (no trailer)
   468→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   469→	if err != nil {
   470→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   471→	}
   472→
   473→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   474→	if err != nil {
   475→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get worktree: %v", err)
   476→	}
   477→
   478→	_, err = worktree.Commit(cleanedMsg, &git.CommitOptions{
   479→		Author: &object.Signature{
   480→			Name:  "E2E Test User",
   481→			Email: "e2e-test@example.com",
   482→			When:  time.Now(),
   483→		},
   484→	})
   485→	if err != nil {
   486→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to commit: %v", err)
   487→	}
   488→
   489→	// Run post-commit hook - since trailer was removed, no condensation should happen
   490→	//nolint:gosec,noctx // test code, args are from trusted test setup, no context needed
   491→	postCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "post-commit")
   492→	postCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   493→	postCmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
   494→		"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".claude"),
   495→		"ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".gemini"),
   496→	)
   497→	if output, err := postCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
   498→		env.T.Logf("post-commit output: %s", output)
   499→	}
   500→}

   531→// RewindPoint mirrors the rewind --list JSON output.
   532→type RewindPoint struct {
   533→	ID               string    `json:"id"`
   534→	Message          string    `json:"message"`
   535→	MetadataDir      string    `json:"metadata_dir"`
   536→	Date             time.Time `json:"date"`
   537→	IsTaskCheckpoint bool      `json:"is_task_checkpoint"`
   538→	ToolUseID        string    `json:"tool_use_id"`
   539→	IsLogsOnly       bool      `json:"is_logs_only"`
   540→	CondensationID   string    `json:"condensation_id"`
   541→}
   542→
   543→// GetRewindPoints returns available rewind points using the CLI.
   544→func (env *TestEnv) GetRewindPoints() []RewindPoint {
   545→	env.T.Helper()
   546→
   547→	//nolint:gosec,noctx // test code, args are static, no context needed
   548→	cmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "rewind", "--list")
   549→	cmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   550→
   551→	output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()
   552→	if err != nil {
   553→		env.T.Fatalf("rewind --list failed: %v\nOutput: %s", err, output)
   554→	}
   555→
   556→	// Parse JSON output
   557→	var jsonPoints []struct {
   558→		ID               string `json:"id"`
   559→		Message          string `json:"message"`
   560→		MetadataDir      string `json:"metadata_dir"`
   561→		Date             string `json:"date"`
   562→		IsTaskCheckpoint bool   `json:"is_task_checkpoint"`
   563→		ToolUseID        string `json:"tool_use_id"`
   564→		IsLogsOnly       bool   `json:"is_logs_only"`
   565→		CondensationID   string `json:"condensation_id"`
   566→	}
   567→
   568→	if err := json.Unmarshal(output, &jsonPoints); err != nil {
   569→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to parse rewind points: %v\nOutput: %s", err, output)
   570→	}
   571→
   572→	points := make([]RewindPoint, len(jsonPoints))
   573→	for i, jp := range jsonPoints {
   574→		//nolint:errcheck // date parsing failure is acceptable, defaults to zero time
   575→		date, _ := time.Parse(time.RFC3339, jp.Date)
   576→		points[i] = RewindPoint{
   577→			ID:               jp.ID,
   578→			Message:          jp.Message,
   579→			MetadataDir:      jp.MetadataDir,
   580→			Date:             date,
   581→			IsTaskCheckpoint: jp.IsTaskCheckpoint,
   582→			ToolUseID:        jp.ToolUseID,
   583→			IsLogsOnly:       jp.IsLogsOnly,
   584→			CondensationID:   jp.CondensationID,
   585→		}
   586→	}
   587→
   588→	return points
   589→}

   608→// BranchExists checks if a branch exists in the repository.
   609→func (env *TestEnv) BranchExists(branchName string) bool {
   610→	env.T.Helper()
   611→
   612→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   613→	if err != nil {
   614→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   615→	}
   616→
   617→	refs, err := repo.References()
   618→	if err != nil {
   619→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get references: %v", err)
   620→	}
   621→
   622→	found := false
   623→	//nolint:errcheck,gosec // ForEach callback doesn't return errors we need to handle
   624→	refs.ForEach(func(ref *plumbing.Reference) error {
   625→		if ref.Name().Short() == branchName {
   626→			found = true
   627→		}
   628→		return nil
   629→	})
   630→
   631→	return found
   632→}
   633→
   634→// ListBranchesWithPrefix returns all branches that start with the given prefix.
   635→func (env *TestEnv) ListBranchesWithPrefix(prefix string) []string {
   636→	env.T.Helper()
   637→
   638→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   639→	if err != nil {
   640→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   641→	}
   642→
   643→	refs, err := repo.References()
   644→	if err != nil {
   645→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get references: %v", err)
   646→	}
   647→
   648→	var branches []string
   649→	//nolint:errcheck,gosec // ForEach callback doesn't return errors we need to handle
   650→	refs.ForEach(func(ref *plumbing.Reference) error {
   651→		name := ref.Name().Short()
   652→		if strings.HasPrefix(name, prefix) {
   653→			branches = append(branches, name)
   654→		}
   655→		return nil
   656→	})
   657→
   658→	return branches
   659→}

   661→// GetCommitMessage returns the commit message for the given commit hash.

   678→
   679→// GetLatestCheckpointIDFromHistory walks backwards from HEAD and returns
   680→// the checkpoint ID from the first commit with an Entire-Checkpoint trailer.
   681→// Returns an error if no checkpoint trailer is found in any commit.
   682→func (env *TestEnv) GetLatestCheckpointIDFromHistory() (string, error) {
   683→	env.T.Helper()
   684→
   685→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   686→	if err != nil {
   687→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   688→	}
   689→
   690→	head, err := repo.Head()
   691→	if err != nil {
   692→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get HEAD: %v", err)
   693→	}
   694→
   695→	commitIter, err := repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{From: head.Hash()})
   696→	if err != nil {
   697→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to iterate commits: %v", err)
   698→	}
   699→
   700→	var checkpointID string
   701→	//nolint:errcheck,gosec // ForEach callback returns error to stop iteration, not a real error
   702→	commitIter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error {
   703→		// Look for Entire-Checkpoint trailer
   704→		for _, line := range strings.Split(c.Message, "\n") {
   705→			line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   706→			if strings.HasPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:") {
   707→				checkpointID = strings.TrimSpace(strings.TrimPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:"))
   708→				return errors.New("stop iteration")
   709→			}
   710→		}
   711→		return nil
   712→	})
   713→
   714→	if checkpointID == "" {
   715→		return "", errors.New("no commit with Entire-Checkpoint trailer found in history")
   716→	}
   717→
   718→	return checkpointID, nil
   719→}

   721→// GetLatestCheckpointID returns the checkpoint ID from HEAD commit, or empty string if none.
   722→// This is a convenience wrapper that doesn't fail the test on missing checkpoint.
   723→func (env *TestEnv) GetLatestCheckpointID() string {
   724→	env.T.Helper()
   725→
   726→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   727→	if err != nil {
   728→		return ""
   729→	}
   730→
   731→	head, err := repo.Head()
   732→	if err != nil {
   733→		return ""
   734→	}
   735→
   736→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(head.Hash())
   737→	if err != nil {
   738→		return ""
   739→	}
   740→
   741→	// Look for Entire-Checkpoint trailer in HEAD commit only
   742→	for _, line := range strings.Split(commit.Message, "\n") {
   743→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   744→		if strings.HasPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:") {
   745→			return strings.TrimSpace(strings.TrimPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:"))
   746→		}
   747→	}
   748→	return ""
   749→}

   751→// safeIDPrefix returns first 12 chars of ID or the full ID if shorter.
   752→// Use this when logging checkpoint IDs to avoid index out of bounds panic.
   753→func safeIDPrefix(id string) string {
   754→	if len(id) >= 12 {
   755→		return id[:12]
   756→	}
   757→	return id
   758→}

   782→// RunAgent runs the agent with the given prompt and returns the result.

   788→		if result.Stderr != "" {

   791→		if result.Stdout != "" {

   793→		}
   794→	}
   795→	//nolint:wrapcheck // test helper, caller handles error
   796→	return result, err
   797→}

   863→// GetCommitCount returns the number of commits on the current branch.
   864→func (env *TestEnv) GetCommitCount() int {

   867→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   868→	if err != nil {

   873→	if err != nil {

   881→

   884→	commitIter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error {
   885→		count++
   886→		return nil
   887→	})
   888→
   889→	return count
   890→}

   892→// GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory walks backwards from HEAD and returns

   894→func (env *TestEnv) GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory() []string {

   902→	head, err := repo.Head()
   903→	if err != nil {
   904→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get HEAD: %v", err)
   905→	}
   906→
   907→	commitIter, err := repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{From: head.Hash()})
   908→	if err != nil {
   909→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to iterate commits: %v", err)

   911→
   912→	var checkpointIDs []string
   913→	//nolint:errcheck,gosec // ForEach callback doesn't return errors we need to handle
   914→	commitIter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error {

   916→			line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   917→			if strings.HasPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:") {
   918→				id := strings.TrimSpace(strings.TrimPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:"))
   919→				checkpointIDs = append(checkpointIDs, id)
   920→			}
   921→		}
   922→		return nil
   923→	})

   925→	return checkpointIDs

   928→// ReadFileFromBranch reads a file's content from a specific branch's tree.
   929→// Returns the content and true if found, empty string and false if not found.
   930→func (env *TestEnv) ReadFileFromBranch(branchName, filePath string) (string, bool) {
   931→	env.T.Helper()
   932→
   933→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   934→	if err != nil {
   935→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   936→	}
   937→
   938→	// Get the branch reference
   939→	ref, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(branchName), true)
   940→	if err != nil {
   941→		// Try as a remote-style ref
   942→		ref, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.ReferenceName("refs/heads/"+branchName), true)
   943→		if err != nil {
   944→			return "", false
   945→		}
   946→	}
   947→
   948→	// Get the commit
   949→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   950→	if err != nil {
   951→		return "", false
   952→	}
   953→
   954→	// Get the tree
   955→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   956→	if err != nil {
   957→		return "", false
   958→	}
   959→
   960→	// Get the file
   961→	file, err := tree.File(filePath)
   962→	if err != nil {
   963→		return "", false
   964→	}
   965→
   966→	// Get the content
   967→	content, err := file.Contents()
   968→	if err != nil {
   969→		return "", false
   970→	}
   971→
   972→	return content, true
   973→}
   974→
   975→// CheckpointValidation contains expected values for checkpoint validation.
   976→type CheckpointValidation struct {
   977→	// CheckpointID is the expected checkpoint ID
   978→	CheckpointID string
   979→
   980→	// Strategy is the expected strategy name (optional)
   981→	Strategy string
   982→
   983→	// FilesTouched are the expected files in files_touched (optional)
   984→	FilesTouched []string
   985→
   986→	// ExpectedPrompts are strings that should appear in prompt.txt (optional)
   987→	ExpectedPrompts []string
   988→
   989→	// ExpectedTranscriptContent are strings that should appear in full.jsonl (optional)
   990→	ExpectedTranscriptContent []string
   991→}
   992→
   993→// ValidateCheckpoint performs comprehensive validation of a checkpoint on the metadata branch.
   994→func (env *TestEnv) ValidateCheckpoint(v CheckpointValidation) {
   995→	env.T.Helper()
   996→
   997→	metadataBranch := "entire/checkpoints/v1"
   998→
   999→	// Compute sharded path: <id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/
  1000→	if len(v.CheckpointID) < 3 {
  1001→		env.T.Fatalf("Checkpoint ID too short: %s", v.CheckpointID)
  1002→	}
  1003→	shardedPath := v.CheckpointID[:2] + "/" + v.CheckpointID[2:]
  1004→
  1005→	// Validate root metadata.json exists and has expected fields
  1006→	summaryPath := shardedPath + "/metadata.json"
  1007→	summaryContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(metadataBranch, summaryPath)
  1008→	if !found {
  1009→		env.T.Errorf("CheckpointSummary not found at %s", summaryPath)
  1010→	} else {
  1011→		var summary map[string]any
  1012→		if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(summaryContent), &summary); err != nil {
  1013→			env.T.Errorf("Failed to parse CheckpointSummary: %v", err)
  1014→		} else {
  1015→			// Validate checkpoint_id
  1016→			if cpID, ok := summary["checkpoint_id"].(string); !ok || cpID != v.CheckpointID {
  1017→				env.T.Errorf("CheckpointSummary.checkpoint_id = %v, want %s", summary["checkpoint_id"], v.CheckpointID)
  1018→			}
  1019→			// Validate strategy if specified
  1020→			if v.Strategy != "" {
  1021→				if strategy, ok := summary["strategy"].(string); !ok || strategy != v.Strategy {
  1022→					env.T.Errorf("CheckpointSummary.strategy = %v, want %s", summary["strategy"], v.Strategy)
  1023→				}
  1024→			}
  1025→			// Validate sessions array exists
  1026→			if sessions, ok := summary["sessions"].([]any); !ok || len(sessions) == 0 {
  1027→				env.T.Error("CheckpointSummary.sessions should have at least one entry")
  1028→			}
  1029→			// Validate files_touched if specified
  1030→			if len(v.FilesTouched) > 0 {
  1031→				if filesTouched, ok := summary["files_touched"].([]any); ok {
  1032→					touchedSet := make(map[string]bool)
  1033→					for _, f := range filesTouched {
  1034→						if s, ok := f.(string); ok {
  1035→							touchedSet[s] = true
  1036→						}
  1037→					}
  1038→					for _, expected := range v.FilesTouched {
  1039→						if !touchedSet[expected] {
  1040→							env.T.Errorf("CheckpointSummary.files_touched missing %q", expected)
  1041→						}
  1042→					}
  1043→				}
  1044→			}
  1045→		}
  1046→	}
  1047→
  1048→	// Validate session metadata.json exists
  1049→	sessionMetadataPath := shardedPath + "/0/metadata.json"
  1050→	sessionContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(metadataBranch, sessionMetadataPath)
  1051→	if !found {
  1052→		env.T.Errorf("Session metadata not found at %s", sessionMetadataPath)
  1053→	} else {
  1054→		var metadata map[string]any
  1055→		if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(sessionContent), &metadata); err != nil {
  1056→			env.T.Errorf("Failed to parse session metadata: %v", err)
  1057→		} else {
  1058→			// Validate checkpoint_id matches
  1059→			if cpID, ok := metadata["checkpoint_id"].(string); !ok || cpID != v.CheckpointID {
  1060→				env.T.Errorf("Session metadata.checkpoint_id = %v, want %s", metadata["checkpoint_id"], v.CheckpointID)
  1061→			}
  1062→			// Validate created_at exists
  1063→			if _, ok := metadata["created_at"].(string); !ok {
  1064→				env.T.Error("Session metadata.created_at should exist")
  1065→			}
  1066→		}
  1067→	}
  1068→
  1069→	// Validate transcript is valid JSONL (only enforce for Claude Code, log warnings for others)
  1070→	transcriptPath := shardedPath + "/0/full.jsonl"
  1071→	transcriptContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(metadataBranch, transcriptPath)
  1072→	if !found {
  1073→		env.T.Errorf("Transcript not found at %s", transcriptPath)
  1074→	} else {
  1075→		// Check each line is valid JSON
  1076→		// Only enforce JSONL format for Claude Code - other agents may have different formats
  1077→		isClaudeCode := env.Agent.Name() == AgentNameClaudeCode
  1078→		lines := strings.Split(transcriptContent, "\n")
  1079→		validLines := 0
  1080→		for i, line := range lines {
  1081→			line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
  1082→			if line == "" {
  1083→				continue
  1084→			}
  1085→			validLines++
  1086→			var obj map[string]any
  1087→			if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &obj); err != nil {
  1088→				if isClaudeCode {
  1089→					env.T.Errorf("Transcript line %d is not valid JSON: %v", i+1, err)
  1090→				}
  1091→				// For non-Claude agents, just log (transcript format may differ)
  1092→			}
  1093→		}
  1094→		if validLines == 0 {
  1095→			env.T.Error("Transcript is empty (no valid JSONL lines)")
  1096→		}
  1097→		// Check expected content
  1098→		for _, expected := range v.ExpectedTranscriptContent {
  1099→			if !strings.Contains(transcriptContent, expected) {
  1100→				env.T.Errorf("Transcript should contain %q", expected)
  1101→			}
  1102→		}
  1103→	}
  1104→
  1105→	// Validate prompt.txt contains expected prompts
  1106→	if len(v.ExpectedPrompts) > 0 {
  1107→		promptPath := shardedPath + "/0/prompt.txt"
  1108→		promptContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(metadataBranch, promptPath)
  1109→		if !found {
  1110→			env.T.Errorf("Prompt file not found at %s", promptPath)
  1111→		} else {
  1112→			for _, expected := range v.ExpectedPrompts {
  1113→				if !strings.Contains(promptContent, expected) {
  1114→					env.T.Errorf("Prompt file should contain %q", expected)
  1115→				}
  1116→			}
  1117→		}
  1118→	}
  1119→
  1120→	// Validate content hash exists and matches transcript
  1121→	hashPath := shardedPath + "/0/content_hash.txt"
  1122→	hashContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(metadataBranch, hashPath)
  1123→	if !found {
  1124→		env.T.Errorf("Content hash not found at %s", hashPath)
  1125→	} else if transcriptContent != "" {
  1126→		// Verify hash matches
  1127→		hash := sha256.Sum256([]byte(transcriptContent))
  1128→		expectedHash := "sha256:" + hex.EncodeToString(hash[:])
  1129→		storedHash := strings.TrimSpace(hashContent)
  1130→		if storedHash != expectedHash {
  1131→			env.T.Errorf("Content hash mismatch:\n  stored:   %s\n  expected: %s", storedHash, expectedHash)
  1132→		}
  1133→	}
  1134→}
  1135→
```

     1→# Plan: GitHub Copilot CLI Agent Integration
     2→
     3→## Context
     4→
     5→The Entire CLI currently integrates with Claude Code (stable) and Gemini CLI (preview) via a generic Agent interface. GitHub Copilot CLI is a standalone terminal agent (`copilot` command) that supports hooks similar to Claude/Gemini. This plan adds Copilot as a third agent integration (preview status).
     6→
     7→**Good news**: All Copilot hooks include common fields `sessionId`, `transcript_path`, and `hookEventName` in their stdin JSON. Additionally, `agentStop` provides TurnEnd support. This means the integration follows the same pattern as Claude/Gemini.
     8→
     9→---
    10→
    11→## Hook Inventory (8 hooks)
    12→
    13→| Copilot Hook | CLI Subcommand | Normalized Event | Notes |
    14→|---|---|---|---|
    15→| `sessionStart` | `session-start` | `SessionStart` | Session begins or resumes |
    16→| `sessionEnd` | `session-end` | `SessionEnd` | Session completes/terminated |
    17→| `userPromptSubmitted` | `user-prompt-submitted` | `TurnStart` | User submits prompt |
    18→| `agentStop` | `agent-stop` | `TurnEnd` | Agent finished responding |
    19→| `subagentStop` | `subagent-stop` | `SubagentEnd` | Subagent completed |
    20→| `preToolUse` | `pre-tool-use` | `nil` | Pass-through |
    21→| `postToolUse` | `post-tool-use` | `nil` | Pass-through |
    22→| `errorOccurred` | `error-occurred` | `nil` | Logging only |
    23→
    24→**Common stdin fields** (all hooks): `timestamp`, `cwd`, `sessionId`, `hookEventName`, `transcript_path`
    25→
    26→**Hook-specific fields**: `source`/`initialPrompt` (sessionStart), `reason` (sessionEnd), `prompt` (userPromptSubmitted), `toolName`/`toolArgs` (preToolUse/postToolUse), `toolResult` (postToolUse), `error` (errorOccurred)
    27→
    28→---
    29→
    30→## Step 0: Verify Hook stdin Format
    31→
    32→**Before writing code**, capture actual stdin JSON to confirm the common fields and `agentStop`/`subagentStop` format:
    33→
    34→```json
    35→{
    36→  "version": 1,
    37→  "hooks": {
    38→    "sessionStart": [{"type": "command", "bash": "cat > /tmp/copilot-session-start.json", "timeoutSec": 10}],
    39→    "agentStop": [{"type": "command", "bash": "cat > /tmp/copilot-agent-stop.json", "timeoutSec": 10}],
    40→    "userPromptSubmitted": [{"type": "command", "bash": "cat > /tmp/copilot-user-prompt.json", "timeoutSec": 10}]
    41→  }
    42→}
    43→```
    44→
    45→Place at `.github/hooks/copilot-setup.json`, run `copilot`, check `/tmp/copilot-*.json`. Confirm `sessionId` and `transcript_path` are present.
    46→
    47→---
    48→
    49→## Step 1: Add Registry Constants
    50→
```

envTimeout != \"\" {\n\t\t\tif parsed, err := time.ParseDuration(envTimeout); err == nil {\n\t\t\t\ttimeout = parsed\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tif timeout == 0 {\n\t\t\ttimeout = 2 * time.Minute\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn &CopilotRunner{\n\t\tModel:   model,\n\t\tTimeout: timeout,\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (r *CopilotRunner) Name() string {\n\treturn AgentNameCopilot\n}\n\n// IsAvailable checks if Copilot CLI is installed and responds to --version.\nfunc (r *CopilotRunner) IsAvailable() (bool, error) {\n\tif _, err := exec.LookPath(\"copilot\"); err != nil {\n\t\treturn false, fmt.Errorf(\"copilot CLI not found in PATH: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 10*time.Second)\n\tdefer cancel()\n\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, \"copilot\", \"--version\")\n\tif err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn false, fmt.Errorf(\"copilot CLI not working: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn true, nil\n}\n\nfunc (r *CopilotRunner) RunPrompt(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string) (*AgentResult, error) {\n\treturn r.RunPromptWithTools(ctx, workDir, prompt, nil)\n}\n\nfunc (r *CopilotRunner) RunPromptWithTools(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string, tools []string) (*AgentResult, error) {\n\targs := []string{\n\t\t\"--model\", r.Model,\n\t\t\"-p\", prompt,\n\t\t\"--allow-all-tools\",\n\t}\n\n\tif len(tools) > 0 {\n\t\targs = append(args, \"--available-tools\")\n\t\targs = append(args, tools...)\n\t}\n\n\tctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, r.Timeout)\n\tdefer cancel()\n\n\t//nolint:gosec // args are constructed from trusted config, not user input\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, \"copilot\", args...)\n\tcmd.Dir = workDir\n\n\tvar stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer\n\tcmd.Stdout = &stdout\n\tcmd.Stderr = &stderr\n\n\tstart := time.Now()\n\terr := cmd.Run()\n\tduration := time.Since(start)\n\n\tresult := &AgentResult{\n\t\tStdout:   stdout.String(),\n\t\tStderr:   stderr.String(),\n\t\tDuration: duration,\n\t}\n\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\texitErr := &exec.ExitError{}\n\t\tif errors.As(err, &exitErr) {\n\t\t\tresult.ExitCode = exitErr.ExitCode()\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tresult.ExitCode = -1\n\t\t}\n\t\t//nolint:wrapcheck // error is from exec.Run, caller can check ExitCode in result\n\t\treturn result, err\n\t}\n\n\tresult.ExitCode = 0\n\treturn result, nil\n}\n"
}

All tests pass, linting is clean. Here's a summary of the changes:

**`cmd/entire/cli/e2e_test/agent_runner.go`:**
- Added `AgentNameCopilot = "github-copilot"` constant
- Added `CopilotRunner` struct with `Model` and `Timeout` fields
- `NewCopilotRunner` defaults: model from `E2E_COPILOT_MODEL` env or `"claude-haiku-4.5"`, timeout from `E2E_TIMEOUT` or 2 minutes
- `IsAvailable()` checks `exec.LookPath("copilot")` + `copilot --version`
- `RunPromptWithTools()` builds `copilot --model <model> -p "<prompt>" --allow-all-tools` (with optional `--available-tools`)
- Registered in `NewAgentRunner` switch for `"github-copilot"`

**`cmd/entire/cli/e2e_test/testenv.go`:**
- Added `ENTIRE_TEST_COPILOT_SESSION_DIR` env var to all 6 hook env blocks (across `GitCommitWithShadowHooks`, `GitCommitStagedWithShadowHooks`, and `GitCommitWithTrailerRemoved`), pointing to `<repoDir>/.copilot-sessions`

Now I see the problem clearly. The key issue is:

```
● Unknown tool name in the tool allowlist: "Write"
● Unknown tool name in the tool allowlist: "Bash"
```

Copilot's `--available-tools` flag expects its own tool names (not Claude Code tool names). The tests that pass are the ones that don't pass specific tools. The tests that fail are the ones where `RunPromptWithTools` is called with Claude Code tool names like `"Write"`, `"Bash"`, `"Edit"`.

Let me check which tests use `RunAgentWithTools`.

> AGENT

The tests pass Claude Code tool names (`"Write"`, `"Bash"`) but Copilot uses different tool names. The solution is to have `CopilotRunner.RunPromptWithTools` translate Claude Code tool names to copilot equivalents, or just ignore the `tools` parameter since `--allow-all-tools` already grants everything.

Let me check how Gemini handles this — it adds its own default tools and merges.

Looking at the Gemini runner, it defines its own `defaultAllowedTools` and merges. For Copilot, we already pass `--allow-all-tools` which auto-approves everything, so `--available-tools` would actually *restrict* the available tools rather than allow them. The `--available-tools` flag in copilot means "only these tools will be available" — it's a restriction, not an allowlist.

The fix: `CopilotRunner` should ignore the `tools` parameter since `--allow-all-tools` already permits everything, and there's no meaningful way to map Claude's tool names to Copilot's.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01JfeEEWqeyg8r4aNsJrisfU
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/gtrrz-victor/wks/cli/cli/cmd/entire/cli/e2e_test/agent_runner.go",
  "old_string": "func (r *CopilotRunner) RunPromptWithTools(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string, tools []string) (*AgentResult, error) {\n\targs := []string{\n\t\t\"--model\", r.Model,\n\t\t\"-p\", prompt,\n\t\t\"--allow-all-tools\",\n\t}\n\n\tif len(tools) > 0 {\n\t\targs = append(args, \"--available-tools\")\n\t\targs = append(args, tools...)\n\t}\n\n\tctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, r.Timeout)",
  "new_string": "func (r *CopilotRunner) RunPromptWithTools(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string, _ []string) (*AgentResult, error) {\n\t// Copilot uses --allow-all-tools for non-interactive mode which grants all permissions.\n\t// The tools parameter is ignored because Copilot tool names differ from Claude Code's\n\t// (e.g., \"bash\" vs \"Bash\", \"create\" vs \"Write\") and --available-tools restricts\n\t// rather than allows, so passing Claude tool names would disable most tools.\n\targs := []string{\n\t\t\"--model\", r.Model,\n\t\t\"-p\", prompt,\n\t\t\"--allow-all-tools\",\n\t}\n\n\tctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, r.Timeout)"
}

## E2E Test Analysis: RunAgent vs RunAgentWithTools

Based on my analysis of the failing e2e tests, here's which ones call RunAgent vs RunAgentWithTools:

### Tests calling RunAgentWithTools (with explicit tools):

1. **TestE2E_AgentCommitsDuringTurn** (line 33)
   - `env.RunAgentWithTools(commitPrompt, []string{"Bash"})`
   - Tools: `Bash` only

2. **TestE2E_Scenario2_AgentCommitsDuringTurn** (line 697)
   - `env.RunAgentWithTools(commitPrompt, []string{"Write", "Bash"})`
   - Tools: `Write`, `Bash`

3. **TestE2E_Scenario3_MultipleGranularCommits** (line 52)
   - `env.RunAgentWithTools(granularCommitPrompt, []string{"Write", "Bash"})`
   - Tools: `Write`, `Bash`

4. **TestE2E_DeletedFiles_CommitDeletion** (line 1112)
   - `env.RunAgentWithTools(prompt, []string{"Write", "Bash"})`
   - Tools: `Write`, `Bash`

5. **TestE2E_AgentCommitsMidTurn_UserCommitsRemainder** (line 1174)
   - `env.RunAgentWithTools(prompt, []string{"Write", "Bash"})`
   - Tools: `Write`, `Bash`

### Tests calling RunAgent (without explicit tools):

1. **TestE2E_Scenario4_UserSplitsCommits** (line 137)
   - `env.RunAgent(multiFilePrompt)`
   - No tools specified

2. **TestE2E_Scenario6_StashSecondPromptUnstashCommitAll** (lines 334, 361)
   - `env.RunAgent(prompt1)` and `env.RunAgent(prompt2)`
   - No tools specified (called twice with different prompts)

3. **TestE2E_Scenario7_PartialStagingSimulated** (line 466)
   - `env.RunAgent(multiLinePrompt)`
   - No tools specified

4. **TestE2E_EndedSession_UserCommitsAfterExit** (line 1046)
   - `env.RunAgent(prompt)`
   - No tools specified

5. **TestE2E_ExistingFiles_SplitCommits** (line 861)
   - `env.RunAgent(prompt)`
   - No tools specified

## Summary

- **5 tests use RunAgentWithTools** with explicit tool specifications (primarily `Write` and `Bash`)
- **5 tests use RunAgent** without specifying tools (relying on defaults)

The tests using RunAgentWithTools are those that require the agent to perform git commits (using Bash) or file writes, while the tests using RunAgent are those that only expect the agent to create/modify files without explicitly committing them mid-turn.

    53→// GetHookNames implements agent.HookHandler by delegating to HookNames.

    58→// InstallHooks installs Copilot hooks in .github/hooks/copilot-setup.json.
    59→// If force is true, removes existing Entire hooks before installing.
    60→// Returns the number of hooks installed.
    61→func (c *CopilotAgent) InstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error) {
    62→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
    63→	if err != nil {
    64→		repoRoot, err = os.Getwd() //nolint:forbidigo // Intentional fallback when RepoRoot() fails (tests run outside git repos)
    65→		if err != nil {
    66→			return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to get current directory: %w", err)
    67→		}
    68→	}
    69→
    70→	configPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".github", "hooks", CopilotConfigFileName)
    71→
    72→	// Read existing config if it exists
    73→	var config hookConfig
    74→	existingData, readErr := os.ReadFile(configPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path
    75→	if readErr == nil {
    76→		if err := json.Unmarshal(existingData, &config); err != nil {
    77→			return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse existing %s: %w", CopilotConfigFileName, err)
    78→		}
    79→	} else {
    80→		config.Version = 1
    81→	}
    82→
    83→	if config.Hooks == nil {
    84→		config.Hooks = make(map[string][]hookEntry)
    85→	}
    86→
    87→	// Define hook command based on localDev mode
    88→	var cmdPrefix string
    89→	if localDev {
    90→		cmdPrefix = "go run ${COPILOT_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks github-copilot "
    91→	} else {
    92→		cmdPrefix = "entire hooks github-copilot "
    93→	}
    94→
    95→	// Check for idempotency BEFORE removing hooks
    96→	if !force {
    97→		configKey := hookNameToConfigKey[HookNameSessionStart]
    98→		expectedCmd := cmdPrefix + HookNameSessionStart
    99→		if entries, ok := config.Hooks[configKey]; ok {
   100→			for _, entry := range entries {
   101→				if entry.Bash == expectedCmd {
   102→					return 0, nil // Already installed with same mode
   103→				}
   104→			}
   105→		}
   106→	}
   107→
   108→	// Remove existing Entire hooks first
   109→	for configKey, entries := range config.Hooks {
   110→		config.Hooks[configKey] = removeEntireHookEntries(entries)
   111→		if len(config.Hooks[configKey]) == 0 {
   112→			delete(config.Hooks, configKey)
   113→		}
   114→	}
   115→
   116→	// Install all 8 hooks
   117→	hookNames := c.HookNames()
   118→	for _, hookName := range hookNames {
   119→		configKey, ok := hookNameToConfigKey[hookName]
   120→		if !ok {
   121→			continue
   122→		}
   123→
   124→		entry := hookEntry{
   125→			Type:       "command",
   126→			Bash:       cmdPrefix + hookName,
   127→			TimeoutSec: 10,
   128→		}
   129→
   130→		config.Hooks[configKey] = append(config.Hooks[configKey], entry)
   131→	}
   132→
   133→	count := len(hookNames)
   134→
   135→	// Write config file
   136→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(configPath), 0o750); err != nil {
   137→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to create .github/hooks directory: %w", err)
   138→	}
   139→
   140→	output, err := json.MarshalIndent(config, "", "  ")
   141→	if err != nil {
   142→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal config: %w", err)
   143→	}
   144→
   145→	if err := os.WriteFile(configPath, output, 0o600); err != nil {
   146→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to write %s: %w", CopilotConfigFileName, err)
   147→	}
   148→
   149→	return count, nil
   150→}
   151→
   152→// UninstallHooks removes Entire hooks from Copilot config.
   153→func (c *CopilotAgent) UninstallHooks() error {
   154→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   155→	if err != nil {
   156→		repoRoot = "."
   157→	}
   158→
   159→	configPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".github", "hooks", CopilotConfigFileName)
   160→	data, err := os.ReadFile(configPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path
   161→	if err != nil {
   162→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // No config file means nothing to uninstall
   163→	}
   164→
   165→	var config hookConfig
   166→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &config); err != nil {
   167→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse %s: %w", CopilotConfigFileName, err)
   168→	}
   169→
   170→	if config.Hooks == nil {
   171→		return nil
   172→	}
   173→
   174→	// Remove Entire hooks from all hook types
   175→	for configKey, entries := range config.Hooks {
   176→		config.Hooks[configKey] = removeEntireHookEntries(entries)
   177→		if len(config.Hooks[configKey]) == 0 {
   178→			delete(config.Hooks, configKey)
   179→		}
   180→	}
   181→
   182→	// Write back
   183→	output, err := json.MarshalIndent(config, "", "  ")
   184→	if err != nil {
   185→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal config: %w", err)
   186→	}
   187→
   188→	if err := os.WriteFile(configPath, output, 0o600); err != nil {
   189→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write %s: %w", CopilotConfigFileName, err)
   190→	}
   191→
   192→	return nil
   193→}

   197→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()

   199→		repoRoot = "."
   200→	}
   201→
   202→	configPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".github", "hooks", CopilotConfigFileName)

   204→	if err != nil {
   205→		return false
   206→	}
   207→
   208→	var config hookConfig
   209→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &config); err != nil {
   210→		return false
   211→	}
   212→
   213→	for _, entries := range config.Hooks {
   214→		for _, entry := range entries {
   215→			if isEntireHook(entry.Bash) {
   216→				return true
   217→			}
   218→		}
   219→	}
   220→
   221→	return false
   222→}
   223→
   224→// GetSupportedHooks returns the hook types Copilot supports.
   225→func (c *CopilotAgent) GetSupportedHooks() []agent.HookType {
   226→	return []agent.HookType{
   227→		agent.HookSessionStart,
   228→		agent.HookSessionEnd,
   229→		agent.HookStop,
   230→		agent.HookUserPromptSubmit,
   231→		agent.HookPreToolUse,
   232→		agent.HookPostToolUse,
   233→	}
   234→}
   235→
   236→// Helper functions
   237→
   238→// isEntireHook checks if a command is an Entire hook
   239→func isEntireHook(command string) bool {
   240→	for _, prefix := range entireHookPrefixes {
   241→		if strings.HasPrefix(command, prefix) {
   242→			return true
   243→		}
   244→	}
   245→	return false
   246→}
   247→
   248→// removeEntireHookEntries removes all Entire hook entries from a slice
   249→func removeEntireHookEntries(entries []hookEntry) []hookEntry {
   250→	result := make([]hookEntry, 0, len(entries))
   251→	for _, entry := range entries {
   252→		if !isEntireHook(entry.Bash) {
   253→			result = append(result, entry)
   254→		}
   255→	}
   256→	return result
   257→}
   258→

    81→// ParseHookInput parses Copilot hook input from stdin.
    82→func (c *CopilotAgent) ParseHookInput(hookType agent.HookType, reader io.Reader) (*agent.HookInput, error) {
    83→	data, err := io.ReadAll(reader)
    84→	if err != nil {
    85→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read input: %w", err)
    86→	}
    87→
    88→	if len(data) == 0 {
    89→		return nil, errors.New("empty input")
    90→	}
    91→
    92→	input := &agent.HookInput{
    93→		HookType:  hookType,
    94→		Timestamp: time.Now(),
    95→		RawData:   make(map[string]any),
    96→	}
    97→
    98→	switch hookType {
    99→	case agent.HookSessionStart:
   100→		var raw sessionStartRaw
   101→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   102→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   103→		}
   104→		input.RawData["cwd"] = raw.Cwd
   105→		input.RawData["source"] = raw.Source
   106→
   107→	case agent.HookSessionEnd:
   108→		var raw sessionEndRaw
   109→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   110→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   111→		}
   112→		input.RawData["cwd"] = raw.Cwd
   113→		input.RawData["reason"] = raw.Reason
   114→
   115→	case agent.HookStop:
   116→		// agentStop is the only hook with sessionId and transcriptPath
   117→		var raw agentStopRaw
   118→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   119→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   120→		}
   121→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   122→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   123→		input.RawData["cwd"] = raw.Cwd
   124→		input.RawData["stopReason"] = raw.StopReason
   125→
   126→	case agent.HookUserPromptSubmit:
   127→		var raw userPromptRaw
   128→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   129→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   130→		}
   131→		input.RawData["cwd"] = raw.Cwd
   132→		if raw.Prompt != "" {
   133→			input.UserPrompt = raw.Prompt
   134→			input.RawData["prompt"] = raw.Prompt
   135→		}
   136→
   137→	case agent.HookPreToolUse, agent.HookPostToolUse:
   138→		var raw toolUseRaw
   139→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   140→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse tool hook input: %w", err)
   141→		}
   142→		input.ToolName = raw.ToolName
   143→		input.ToolInput = raw.ToolArgs
   144→		if hookType == agent.HookPostToolUse {
   145→			input.ToolResponse = raw.ToolResult
   146→		}
   147→		input.RawData["cwd"] = raw.Cwd
   148→	}
   149→
   150→	return input, nil
   151→}
   152→
   153→// GetSessionID extracts the session ID from hook input.
   154→func (c *CopilotAgent) GetSessionID(input *agent.HookInput) string {
   155→	return input.SessionID
   156→}
   157→
   158→// ProtectedDirs returns directories that Copilot uses.
   159→// .github is a shared directory (not solely ours), so we don't protect it.
   160→func (c *CopilotAgent) ProtectedDirs() []string { return []string{} }
   161→
   162→// GetSessionDir returns the directory where Copilot stores session transcripts.
   163→func (c *CopilotAgent) GetSessionDir(_ string) (string, error) {
   164→	if override := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_COPILOT_SESSION_DIR"); override != "" {
   165→		return override, nil
   166→	}
   167→
   168→	homeDir, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   169→	if err != nil {
   170→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get home directory: %w", err)
   171→	}
   172→
   173→	return filepath.Join(homeDir, ".copilot", "session-state"), nil
   174→}
   175→
   176→// ResolveSessionFile returns the path to a Copilot session file.
   177→func (c *CopilotAgent) ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string {
   178→	return filepath.Join(sessionDir, agentSessionID, "events.jsonl")
   179→}
   180→
   181→// ReadSession reads a session from Copilot's storage.
   182→func (c *CopilotAgent) ReadSession(input *agent.HookInput) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   183→	if input.SessionRef == "" {
   184→		return nil, errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   185→	}
   186→
   187→	data, err := os.ReadFile(input.SessionRef)
   188→	if err != nil {
   189→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   190→	}
   191→
   192→	return &agent.AgentSession{
   193→		SessionID:  input.SessionID,
   194→		AgentName:  c.Name(),
   195→		SessionRef: input.SessionRef,
   196→		StartTime:  time.Now(),
   197→		NativeData: data,
   198→	}, nil
   199→}
   200→
   201→// WriteSession writes a session to Copilot's storage.
   202→func (c *CopilotAgent) WriteSession(session *agent.AgentSession) error {
   203→	if session == nil {
   204→		return errors.New("session is nil")
   205→	}
   206→
   207→	if session.AgentName != "" && session.AgentName != c.Name() {
   208→		return fmt.Errorf("session belongs to agent %q, not %q", session.AgentName, c.Name())
   209→	}
   210→
   211→	if session.SessionRef == "" {
   212→		return errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   213→	}
   214→
   215→	if len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   216→		return errors.New("session has no native data to write")
   217→	}
   218→
   219→	if err := os.WriteFile(session.SessionRef, session.NativeData, 0o600); err != nil {
   220→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", err)
   221→	}
   222→
   223→	return nil
   224→}
   225→
   226→// FormatResumeCommand returns the command to resume a Copilot session.
   227→func (c *CopilotAgent) FormatResumeCommand(_ string) string {
   228→	return "copilot --resume"
   229→}

> AGENT

All 5 failures (6th `ExistingFiles_ModifyAndCommit` might have been flaky) share the same pattern: **"Transcript not found at .../full.jsonl"** and **"Content hash not found"**. This is a condensation issue where the transcript isn't being carried forward to the `entire/checkpoints/v1` branch on subsequent commits.

These are all multi-commit scenarios where the second (or third) commit's checkpoint lacks transcript data. Let me investigate the condensation flow for copilot.

    30→// listCheckpoints returns all checkpoints from the metadata branch.
    31→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.ListCommitted() for reading from entire/checkpoints/v1.
    32→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) listCheckpoints() ([]CheckpointInfo, error) {
    33→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
    34→	if err != nil {
    35→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
    36→	}
    37→
    38→	committed, err := store.ListCommitted(context.Background())
    39→	if err != nil {
    40→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to list committed checkpoints: %w", err)
    41→	}
    42→
    43→	// Convert from checkpoint.CommittedInfo to strategy.CheckpointInfo
    44→	result := make([]CheckpointInfo, 0, len(committed))
    45→	for _, c := range committed {
    46→		result = append(result, CheckpointInfo{
    47→			CheckpointID:     c.CheckpointID,
    48→			SessionID:        c.SessionID,
    49→			CreatedAt:        c.CreatedAt,
    50→			CheckpointsCount: c.CheckpointsCount,
    51→			FilesTouched:     c.FilesTouched,
    52→			Agent:            c.Agent,
    53→			IsTask:           c.IsTask,
    54→			ToolUseID:        c.ToolUseID,
    55→			SessionCount:     c.SessionCount,
    56→			SessionIDs:       c.SessionIDs,
    57→		})
    58→	}
    59→
    60→	return result, nil
    61→}
    62→
    63→// getCheckpointsForSession returns all checkpoints for a session ID.
    64→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getCheckpointsForSession(sessionID string) ([]CheckpointInfo, error) {
    65→	all, err := s.listCheckpoints()
    66→	if err != nil {
    67→		return nil, err
    68→	}
    69→
    70→	var result []CheckpointInfo
    71→	for _, cp := range all {
    72→		if cp.SessionID == sessionID || strings.HasPrefix(cp.SessionID, sessionID) {
    73→			result = append(result, cp)
    74→		}
    75→	}
    76→
    77→	if len(result) == 0 {
    78→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no checkpoints for session: %s", sessionID)
    79→	}
    80→
    81→	return result, nil
    82→}

    84→// getCheckpointLog returns the transcript for a specific checkpoint ID.
    85→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.ReadCommitted() for reading from entire/checkpoints/v1.
    86→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getCheckpointLog(checkpointID id.CheckpointID) ([]byte, error) {
    87→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
    88→	if err != nil {
    89→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
    90→	}
    91→
    92→	content, err := store.ReadLatestSessionContent(context.Background(), checkpointID)
    93→	if err != nil {
    94→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint: %w", err)
    95→	}
    96→	if content == nil {
    97→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("checkpoint not found: %s", checkpointID)
    98→	}
    99→	if len(content.Transcript) == 0 {
   100→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no transcript found for checkpoint: %s", checkpointID)
   101→	}
   102→
   103→	return content.Transcript, nil
   104→}
