  1142→	if state.AgentType == agent.AgentTypeClaudeCode {
  1143→		subagentsDir := filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(state.TranscriptPath), state.SessionID, "subagents")
  1144→		allFiles, extractErr := claudecode.ExtractAllModifiedFiles(state.TranscriptPath, offset, subagentsDir)
  1145→		if extractErr != nil {
  1146→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "extractModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript: extraction failed",
  1147→				slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1148→				slog.String("error", extractErr.Error()),
  1149→			)
  1150→		} else {
  1151→			modifiedFiles = allFiles
  1152→		}
  1153→	} else {
  1154→		files, _, err := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(state.TranscriptPath, offset)
  1155→		if err != nil {
  1156→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "extractModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript: main transcript extraction failed",
  1157→				slog.String("transcript_path", state.TranscriptPath),
  1158→				slog.Any("error", err),
  1159→			)
  1160→		} else {
  1161→			modifiedFiles = files
  1162→		}
  1163→	}
  1164→
  1165→	if len(modifiedFiles) == 0 {
  1166→		return nil
  1167→	}
  1168→
  1169→	// Normalize to repo-relative paths.
  1170→	// Transcript tool_use entries contain absolute paths (e.g., /Users/alex/project/src/main.go)
  1171→	// but getStagedFiles/committedFiles use repo-relative paths (e.g., src/main.go).
  1172→	basePath := state.WorktreePath
  1173→	if basePath == "" {
  1174→		if wp, wpErr := GetWorktreePath(); wpErr == nil {
  1175→			basePath = wp
  1176→		}
  1177→	}
  1178→	if basePath != "" {
  1179→		normalized := make([]string, 0, len(modifiedFiles))
  1180→		for _, f := range modifiedFiles {
  1181→			if rel := paths.ToRelativePath(f, basePath); rel != "" {
  1182→				normalized = append(normalized, rel)
  1183→			} else {
  1184→				normalized = append(normalized, f)
  1185→			}
  1186→		}
  1187→		modifiedFiles = normalized
  1188→	}
  1189→
  1190→	return modifiedFiles
  1191→}
  1192→
  1193→// addTrailerForAgentCommit handles the fast path when an agent is committing
  1194→// (ACTIVE session + no TTY). Generates a checkpoint ID and adds the trailer
  1195→// directly, bypassing content detection and interactive prompts.
  1196→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) addTrailerForAgentCommit(logCtx context.Context, commitMsgFile string, state *SessionState, source string) error {
  1197→	cpID, err := id.Generate()
  1198→	if err != nil {
  1199→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
  1200→	}
  1201→
  1202→	content, err := os.ReadFile(commitMsgFile) //nolint:gosec // commitMsgFile is provided by git hook
  1203→	if err != nil {
  1204→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
  1205→	}
  1206→
  1207→	message := string(content)
  1208→
  1209→	// Don't add if trailer already exists
  1210→	if _, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(message); found {
  1211→		return nil
  1212→	}
  1213→
  1214→	message = addCheckpointTrailer(message, cpID)
  1215→
  1216→	logging.Info(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: agent commit trailer added",
  1217→		slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
  1218→		slog.String("source", source),
  1219→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", cpID.String()),
  1220→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1221→	)
  1222→
  1223→	if err := os.WriteFile(commitMsgFile, []byte(message), 0o600); err != nil {
  1224→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
  1225→	}
  1226→	return nil
  1227→}
  1228→
  1229→// addCheckpointTrailer adds the Entire-Checkpoint trailer to a commit message.
  1230→// Handles proper trailer formatting (blank line before trailers if needed).
  1231→func addCheckpointTrailer(message string, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) string {
  1232→	trailer := trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ": " + checkpointID.String()
  1233→
  1234→	// If message already ends with trailers (lines starting with key:), just append
  1235→	// Otherwise, add a blank line first
  1236→	lines := strings.Split(strings.TrimRight(message, "\n"), "\n")
  1237→
  1238→	// Check if the message already ends with a trailer paragraph.
  1239→	// Git trailers must be in a separate paragraph (preceded by a blank line).
  1240→	// A single-paragraph message (e.g., just a subject line) cannot have trailers,
  1241→	// even if the subject contains ": " (like conventional commits: "docs: Add foo").
  1242→	//
  1243→	// Scan from the bottom: find the last paragraph of non-comment content,
  1244→	// then check if it looks like trailers AND has a blank line above it.
  1245→	hasTrailers := false
  1246→	i := len(lines) - 1
  1247→
  1248→	// Skip trailing comment lines
  1249→	for i >= 0 && strings.HasPrefix(strings.TrimSpace(lines[i]), "#") {
  1250→		i--
  1251→	}
  1252→
  1253→	// Check if the last non-comment line looks like a trailer
  1254→	if i >= 0 {
  1255→		line := strings.TrimSpace(lines[i])
  1256→		if line != "" && strings.Contains(line, ": ") {
  1257→			// Found a trailer-like line. Now scan upward past the trailer block
  1258→			// to verify there's a blank line (paragraph separator) above it.
  1259→			for i > 0 {
  1260→				i--
  1261→				above := strings.TrimSpace(lines[i])
  1262→				if strings.HasPrefix(above, "#") {
  1263→					continue
  1264→				}
  1265→				if above == "" {
  1266→					// Blank line found above trailer block — real trailers
  1267→					hasTrailers = true
  1268→					break
  1269→				}
  1270→				if !strings.Contains(above, ": ") {
  1271→					// Non-trailer, non-blank line — this is message body, not trailers
  1272→					break
  1273→				}
  1274→				// Another trailer-like line, keep scanning upward
  1275→			}
  1276→		}
  1277→	}
  1278→
  1279→	if hasTrailers {
  1280→		// Append trailer directly
  1281→		return strings.TrimRight(message, "\n") + "\n" + trailer + "\n"
  1282→	}
  1283→
  1284→	// Add blank line before trailer
  1285→	return strings.TrimRight(message, "\n") + "\n\n" + trailer + "\n"
  1286→}
  1287→
  1288→// addCheckpointTrailerWithComment adds the Entire-Checkpoint trailer with an explanatory comment.
  1289→// The trailer is placed above the git comment block but below the user's message area,
  1290→// with a comment explaining that the user can remove it if they don't want to link the commit
  1291→// to the agent session. If prompt is non-empty, it's shown as context.
  1292→func addCheckpointTrailerWithComment(message string, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, agentName, prompt string) string {
  1293→	trailer := trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ": " + checkpointID.String()
  1294→	commentLines := []string{
  1295→		"# Remove the Entire-Checkpoint trailer above if you don't want to link this commit to " + agentName + " session context.",
  1296→	}
  1297→	if prompt != "" {
  1298→		commentLines = append(commentLines, "# Last Prompt: "+prompt)
  1299→	}
  1300→	commentLines = append(commentLines, "# The trailer will be added to your next commit based on this branch.")
  1301→	comment := strings.Join(commentLines, "\n")
  1302→
  1303→	lines := strings.Split(message, "\n")
  1304→
  1305→	// Find where the git comment block starts (first # line)
  1306→	commentStart := -1
  1307→	for i, line := range lines {
  1308→		if strings.HasPrefix(line, "#") {
  1309→			commentStart = i
  1310→			break
  1311→		}
  1312→	}
  1313→
  1314→	if commentStart == -1 {
  1315→		// No git comments, append trailer at the end
  1316→		return strings.TrimRight(message, "\n") + "\n\n" + trailer + "\n" + comment + "\n"
  1317→	}
  1318→
  1319→	// Split into user content and git comments
  1320→	userContent := strings.Join(lines[:commentStart], "\n")
  1321→	gitComments := strings.Join(lines[commentStart:], "\n")
  1322→
  1323→	// Build result: user content, blank line, trailer, comment, blank line, git comments
  1324→	userContent = strings.TrimRight(userContent, "\n")
  1325→	if userContent == "" {
  1326→		// No user content yet - leave space for them to type, then trailer
  1327→		// Two newlines: first for user's message line, second for blank separator
  1328→		return "\n\n" + trailer + "\n" + comment + "\n\n" + gitComments
  1329→	}
  1330→	return userContent + "\n\n" + trailer + "\n" + comment + "\n\n" + gitComments
  1331→}
  1332→
  1333→// InitializeSession creates session state for a new session or updates an existing one.
  1334→// This implements the optional SessionInitializer interface.
  1335→// Called during UserPromptSubmit to allow git hooks to detect active sessions.
  1336→//
  1337→// If the session already exists and HEAD has moved (e.g., user committed), updates
  1338→// BaseCommit to the new HEAD so future checkpoints go to the correct shadow branch.
  1339→//
  1340→// If there's an existing shadow branch with commits from a different session ID,
  1341→// returns a SessionIDConflictError to prevent orphaning existing session work.
  1342→//
  1343→// agentType is the human-readable name of the agent (e.g., "Claude Code").
  1344→// transcriptPath is the path to the live transcript file (for mid-session commit detection).
  1345→// userPrompt is the user's prompt text (stored truncated as FirstPrompt for display).
  1346→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) InitializeSession(sessionID string, agentType agent.AgentType, transcriptPath string, userPrompt string) error {
  1347→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
  1348→	if err != nil {
  1349→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
  1350→	}
  1351→
  1352→	// Check if session already exists
  1353→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
  1354→	if err != nil {
  1355→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to check session state: %w", err)
  1356→	}
  1357→
  1358→	if state != nil && state.BaseCommit != "" {
  1359→		// Session is fully initialized — apply phase transition for TurnStart.
  1360→		if transErr := TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventTurnStart, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
  1361→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: turn start transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
  1362→		}
  1363→
  1364→		// Generate a new TurnID for each turn (correlates carry-forward checkpoints)
  1365→		turnID, err := id.Generate()
  1366→		if err != nil {
  1367→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate turn ID: %w", err)
  1368→		}
  1369→		state.TurnID = turnID.String()
  1370→
  1371→		// Backfill AgentType if empty or set to the generic default "Agent"
  1372→		if !isSpecificAgentType(state.AgentType) && agentType != "" {
  1373→			state.AgentType = agentType
  1374→		}
  1375→
  1376→		// Backfill FirstPrompt if empty (for sessions created before the first_prompt field was added)
  1377→		if state.FirstPrompt == "" && userPrompt != "" {
  1378→			state.FirstPrompt = truncatePromptForStorage(userPrompt)
  1379→		}
  1380→
  1381→		// Update transcript path if provided (may change on session resume)
  1382→		if transcriptPath != "" && state.TranscriptPath != transcriptPath {
  1383→			state.TranscriptPath = transcriptPath
  1384→		}
  1385→
  1386→		// Clear checkpoint IDs on every new prompt.
  1387→		// LastCheckpointID is set during PostCommit, cleared at new prompt.
  1388→		// TurnCheckpointIDs tracks mid-turn checkpoints for stop-time finalization.
  1389→		state.LastCheckpointID = ""
  1390→		state.TurnCheckpointIDs = nil
  1391→
  1392→		// Calculate attribution at prompt start (BEFORE agent makes any changes)
  1393→		// This captures user edits since the last checkpoint (or base commit for first prompt).
  1394→		// IMPORTANT: Always calculate attribution, even for the first checkpoint, to capture
  1395→		// user edits made before the first prompt. The inner CalculatePromptAttribution handles
  1396→		// nil lastCheckpointTree by falling back to baseTree.
  1397→		promptAttr := s.calculatePromptAttributionAtStart(repo, state)
  1398→		state.PendingPromptAttribution = &promptAttr
  1399→
  1400→		// Check if HEAD has moved (user pulled/rebased or committed)
  1401→		// migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded handles renaming the shadow branch and updating state.BaseCommit
  1402→		if _, err := s.migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded(repo, state); err != nil {
  1403→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to check/migrate shadow branch: %w", err)
  1404→		}
  1405→
  1406→		if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
  1407→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to update session state: %w", err)
  1408→		}
  1409→		return nil
  1410→	}
  1411→	// If state exists but BaseCommit is empty, it's a partial state from concurrent warning
  1412→	// Continue below to properly initialize it
  1413→
  1414→	// Initialize new session
  1415→	state, err = s.initializeSession(repo, sessionID, agentType, transcriptPath, userPrompt)
  1416→	if err != nil {
  1417→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to initialize session: %w", err)
  1418→	}
  1419→
  1420→	// Apply phase transition: new session starts as ACTIVE.
  1421→	if transErr := TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventTurnStart, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
  1422→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: turn start transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
  1423→	}
  1424→
  1425→	// Calculate attribution for pre-prompt edits
  1426→	// This captures any user edits made before the first prompt
  1427→	promptAttr := s.calculatePromptAttributionAtStart(repo, state)
  1428→	state.PendingPromptAttribution = &promptAttr
  1429→	if err = s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
  1430→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save attribution: %w", err)
  1431→	}
  1432→
  1433→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Initialized shadow session: %s\n", sessionID)
  1434→	return nil
  1435→}
  1436→
  1437→// calculatePromptAttributionAtStart calculates attribution at prompt start (before agent runs).
  1438→// This captures user changes since the last checkpoint - no filtering needed since
  1439→// the agent hasn't made any changes yet.
  1440→//
  1441→// IMPORTANT: This reads from the worktree (not staging area) to match what WriteTemporary
  1442→// captures in checkpoints. If we read staged content but checkpoints capture worktree content,
  1443→// unstaged changes would be in the checkpoint but not counted in PromptAttribution, causing
  1444→// them to be incorrectly attributed to the agent later.
  1445→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) calculatePromptAttributionAtStart(
  1446→	repo *git.Repository,
  1447→	state *SessionState,
  1448→) PromptAttribution {
  1449→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "attribution")
  1450→	nextCheckpointNum := state.StepCount + 1
  1451→	result := PromptAttribution{CheckpointNumber: nextCheckpointNum}
  1452→
  1453→	// Get last checkpoint tree from shadow branch (if it exists)
  1454→	// For the first checkpoint, no shadow branch exists yet - this is fine,
  1455→	// CalculatePromptAttribution will use baseTree as the reference instead.
  1456→	var lastCheckpointTree *object.Tree
  1457→	shadowBranchName := checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
  1458→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
  1459→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
  1460→	if err != nil {
  1461→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution: no shadow branch yet (first checkpoint)",
  1462→			slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName))
  1463→		// Continue with lastCheckpointTree = nil
  1464→	} else {
  1465→		shadowCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
  1466→		if err != nil {
  1467→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution: failed to get shadow commit",
  1468→				slog.String("shadow_ref", ref.Hash().String()),
  1469→				slog.String("error", err.Error()))
  1470→			// Continue with lastCheckpointTree = nil
  1471→		} else {
  1472→			lastCheckpointTree, err = shadowCommit.Tree()
  1473→			if err != nil {
  1474→				logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution: failed to get shadow tree",
  1475→					slog.String("error", err.Error()))
  1476→				// Continue with lastCheckpointTree = nil
  1477→			}
  1478→		}
  1479→	}
  1480→
  1481→	// Get base tree for agent lines calculation
  1482→	var baseTree *object.Tree
  1483→	if baseCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(plumbing.NewHash(state.BaseCommit)); err == nil {
  1484→		if tree, treeErr := baseCommit.Tree(); treeErr == nil {
  1485→			baseTree = tree
  1486→		} else {
  1487→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution: base tree unavailable",
  1488→				slog.String("error", treeErr.Error()))
  1489→		}
  1490→	} else {
  1491→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution: base commit unavailable",
  1492→			slog.String("base_commit", state.BaseCommit),
  1493→			slog.String("error", err.Error()))
  1494→	}
  1495→
  1496→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
  1497→	if err != nil {
  1498→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution skipped: failed to get worktree",
  1499→			slog.String("error", err.Error()))
  1500→		return result
  1501→	}
  1502→
  1503→	// Get worktree status to find ALL changed files
  1504→	status, err := worktree.Status()
  1505→	if err != nil {
  1506→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution skipped: failed to get worktree status",
  1507→			slog.String("error", err.Error()))
  1508→		return result
  1509→	}
  1510→
  1511→	worktreeRoot := worktree.Filesystem.Root()
  1512→
  1513→	// Build map of changed files with their worktree content
  1514→	// IMPORTANT: We read from worktree (not staging area) to match what WriteTemporary
  1515→	// captures in checkpoints. This ensures attribution is consistent.
  1516→	changedFiles := make(map[string]string)
  1517→	for filePath, fileStatus := range status {
  1518→		// Skip unmodified files
  1519→		if fileStatus.Worktree == git.Unmodified && fileStatus.Staging == git.Unmodified {
  1520→			continue
  1521→		}
  1522→		// Skip .entire metadata directory (session data, not user code)
  1523→		if strings.HasPrefix(filePath, paths.EntireMetadataDir+"/") || strings.HasPrefix(filePath, ".entire/") {
  1524→			continue
  1525→		}
  1526→
  1527→		// Always read from worktree to match checkpoint behavior
  1528→		fullPath := filepath.Join(worktreeRoot, filePath)
  1529→		var content string
  1530→		if data, err := os.ReadFile(fullPath); err == nil { //nolint:gosec // filePath is from git worktree status
  1531→			// Use git's binary detection algorithm (matches getFileContent behavior).
  1532→			// Binary files are excluded from line-based attribution calculations.
  1533→			isBinary, binErr := binary.IsBinary(bytes.NewReader(data))
  1534→			if binErr == nil && !isBinary {
  1535→				content = string(data)
  1536→			}
  1537→		}
  1538→		// else: file deleted, unreadable, or binary - content remains empty string
  1539→
  1540→		changedFiles[filePath] = content
  1541→	}
  1542→
  1543→	// Use CalculatePromptAttribution from manual_commit_attribution.go
  1544→	result = CalculatePromptAttribution(baseTree, lastCheckpointTree, changedFiles, nextCheckpointNum)
  1545→
  1546→	return result
  1547→}
  1548→
  1549→// getStagedFiles returns a list of files staged for commit.
  1550→func getStagedFiles(repo *git.Repository) []string {
  1551→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
  1552→	if err != nil {
  1553→		return nil
  1554→	}
  1555→
  1556→	status, err := worktree.Status()
  1557→	if err != nil {
  1558→		return nil
  1559→	}
  1560→
  1561→	var staged []string
  1562→	for path, fileStatus := range status {
  1563→		// Check if file is staged (in index)
  1564→		if fileStatus.Staging != git.Unmodified && fileStatus.Staging != git.Untracked {
  1565→			staged = append(staged, path)
  1566→		}
  1567→	}
  1568→	return staged
  1569→}
  1570→
  1571→// getLastPrompt retrieves the most recent user prompt from a session's shadow branch.
  1572→// Returns empty string if no prompt can be retrieved.
  1573→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getLastPrompt(repo *git.Repository, state *SessionState) string {
  1574→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
  1575→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
  1576→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
  1577→	if err != nil {
  1578→		return ""
  1579→	}
  1580→
  1581→	// Extract session data to get prompts for commit message generation
  1582→	// Pass agent type to handle different transcript formats (JSONL for Claude, JSON for Gemini)
  1583→	// Pass 0 for checkpointTranscriptStart since we're extracting all prompts, not calculating token usage
  1584→	sessionData, err := s.extractSessionData(repo, ref.Hash(), state.SessionID, nil, state.AgentType, "", 0)
  1585→	if err != nil || len(sessionData.Prompts) == 0 {
  1586→		return ""
  1587→	}
  1588→
  1589→	// Return the last prompt (most recent work before commit)
  1590→	return sessionData.Prompts[len(sessionData.Prompts)-1]
  1591→}
  1592→
  1593→// HandleTurnEnd dispatches strategy-specific actions emitted when an agent turn ends.
  1594→// The primary job is to finalize all checkpoints from this turn with the full transcript.
  1595→//
  1596→// During a turn, PostCommit writes provisional transcript data (whatever was available
  1597→// at commit time). HandleTurnEnd replaces that with the complete session transcript
  1598→// (from prompt to stop event), ensuring every checkpoint has the full context.
  1599→//
  1600→//nolint:unparam // error return required by interface but hooks must return nil
  1601→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) HandleTurnEnd(state *SessionState) error {
  1602→	// Finalize all checkpoints from this turn with the full transcript.
  1603→	//
  1604→	// IMPORTANT: This is best-effort - errors are logged but don't fail the hook.
  1605→	// Failing here would prevent session cleanup and could leave state inconsistent.
  1606→	// The provisional transcript from PostCommit is already persisted, so the
  1607→	// checkpoint isn't lost - it just won't have the complete transcript.
  1608→	errCount := s.finalizeAllTurnCheckpoints(state)
  1609→	if errCount > 0 {
  1610→		logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
  1611→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "HandleTurnEnd completed with errors (best-effort)",
  1612→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1613→			slog.Int("error_count", errCount),
  1614→		)
  1615→	}
  1616→	return nil
  1617→}
  1618→
  1619→// finalizeAllTurnCheckpoints replaces the provisional transcript in each checkpoint
  1620→// created during this turn with the full session transcript.
  1621→//
  1622→// This is called at turn end (stop hook). During the turn, PostCommit wrote whatever
  1623→// transcript was available at commit time. Now we have the complete transcript and
  1624→// replace it so every checkpoint has the full prompt-to-stop context.
  1625→//
  1626→// Returns the number of errors encountered (best-effort: continues processing on error).
  1627→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) finalizeAllTurnCheckpoints(state *SessionState) int {
  1628→	if len(state.TurnCheckpointIDs) == 0 {
  1629→		return 0 // No mid-turn commits to finalize
  1630→	}
  1631→
  1632→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
  1633→
  1634→	logging.Info(logCtx, "finalizing turn checkpoints with full transcript",
  1635→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1636→		slog.Int("checkpoint_count", len(state.TurnCheckpointIDs)),
  1637→	)
  1638→
  1639→	errCount := 0
  1640→
  1641→	// Read full transcript from live transcript file
  1642→	if state.TranscriptPath == "" {
  1643→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "finalize: no transcript path, skipping",
  1644→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1645→		)
  1646→		state.TurnCheckpointIDs = nil
  1647→		return 1 // Count as error - all checkpoints will be skipped
  1648→	}
  1649→
  1650→	fullTranscript, err := os.ReadFile(state.TranscriptPath)
  1651→	if err != nil || len(fullTranscript) == 0 {
  1652→		msg := "finalize: empty transcript, skipping"
  1653→		if err != nil {
  1654→			msg = "finalize: failed to read transcript, skipping"
  1655→		}
  1656→		logging.Warn(logCtx, msg,
  1657→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1658→			slog.String("transcript_path", state.TranscriptPath),
  1659→			slog.Any("error", err),
  1660→		)
  1661→		state.TurnCheckpointIDs = nil
  1662→		return 1 // Count as error - all checkpoints will be skipped
  1663→	}
  1664→
  1665→	// Extract prompts and context from the full transcript
  1666→	prompts := extractUserPrompts(state.AgentType, string(fullTranscript))
  1667→	contextBytes := generateContextFromPrompts(prompts)
  1668→
  1669→	// Redact secrets before writing — matches WriteCommitted behavior.
  1670→	// The live transcript on disk contains raw content; redaction must happen
  1671→	// before anything is persisted to the metadata branch.
  1672→	fullTranscript, err = redact.JSONLBytes(fullTranscript)
  1673→	if err != nil {
  1674→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "finalize: transcript redaction failed, skipping",
  1675→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1676→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
  1677→		)
  1678→		state.TurnCheckpointIDs = nil
  1679→		return 1 // Count as error - all checkpoints will be skipped
  1680→	}
  1681→	for i, p := range prompts {
  1682→		prompts[i] = redact.String(p)
  1683→	}
  1684→	contextBytes = redact.Bytes(contextBytes)
  1685→
  1686→	// Open repository and create checkpoint store
  1687→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
  1688→	if err != nil {
  1689→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "finalize: failed to open repository",
  1690→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
  1691→		)
  1692→		state.TurnCheckpointIDs = nil
  1693→		return 1 // Count as error - all checkpoints will be skipped
  1694→	}
  1695→	store := checkpoint.NewGitStore(repo)
  1696→
  1697→	// Update each checkpoint with the full transcript
  1698→	for _, cpIDStr := range state.TurnCheckpointIDs {
  1699→		cpID, parseErr := id.NewCheckpointID(cpIDStr)
  1700→		if parseErr != nil {
  1701→			logging.Warn(logCtx, "finalize: invalid checkpoint ID, skipping",
  1702→				slog.String("checkpoint_id", cpIDStr),
  1703→				slog.String("error", parseErr.Error()),
  1704→			)
  1705→			errCount++
  1706→			continue
  1707→		}
  1708→
  1709→		updateErr := store.UpdateCommitted(context.Background(), checkpoint.UpdateCommittedOptions{
  1710→			CheckpointID: cpID,
  1711→			SessionID:    state.SessionID,
  1712→			Transcript:   fullTranscript,
  1713→			Prompts:      prompts,
  1714→			Context:      contextBytes,
  1715→			Agent:        state.AgentType,
  1716→		})
  1717→		if updateErr != nil {
  1718→			logging.Warn(logCtx, "finalize: failed to update checkpoint",
  1719→				slog.String("checkpoint_id", cpIDStr),
  1720→				slog.String("error", updateErr.Error()),
  1721→			)
  1722→			errCount++
  1723→			continue
  1724→		}
  1725→
  1726→		logging.Info(logCtx, "finalize: checkpoint updated with full transcript",
  1727→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", cpIDStr),
  1728→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1729→		)
  1730→	}
  1731→
  1732→	// Clear turn checkpoint IDs. Do NOT update CheckpointTranscriptStart here — it was
  1733→	// already set correctly by PostCommit: condenseAndUpdateState sets it to the total
  1734→	// transcript lines when condensing, and carryForwardToNewShadowBranch resets it to 0
  1735→	// when carry-forward is active. Overwriting here would break carry-forward by making
  1736→	// sessionHasNewContent think the transcript is fully consumed (no growth).
  1737→	state.TurnCheckpointIDs = nil
  1738→
  1739→	return errCount
  1740→}
  1741→
  1742→// filesChangedInCommit returns the set of files changed in a commit by diffing against its parent.
  1743→func filesChangedInCommit(commit *object.Commit) map[string]struct{} {
  1744→	result := make(map[string]struct{})
  1745→
  1746→	commitTree, err := commit.Tree()
  1747→	if err != nil {
  1748→		return result
  1749→	}
  1750→
  1751→	var parentTree *object.Tree
  1752→	if commit.NumParents() > 0 {
  1753→		parent, err := commit.Parent(0)
  1754→		if err != nil {
  1755→			return result
  1756→		}
  1757→		parentTree, err = parent.Tree()
  1758→		if err != nil {
  1759→			return result
  1760→		}
  1761→	}
  1762→
  1763→	if parentTree == nil {
  1764→		// Initial commit — all files are new
  1765→		if iterErr := commitTree.Files().ForEach(func(f *object.File) error {
  1766→			result[f.Name] = struct{}{}
  1767→			return nil
  1768→		}); iterErr != nil {
  1769→			return result
  1770→		}
  1771→		return result
  1772→	}
  1773→
  1774→	changes, err := parentTree.Diff(commitTree)
  1775→	if err != nil {
  1776→		return result
  1777→	}
  1778→	for _, change := range changes {
  1779→		name := change.To.Name
  1780→		if name == "" {
  1781→			name = change.From.Name
  1782→		}
  1783→		result[name] = struct{}{}
  1784→	}
  1785→	return result
  1786→}
  1787→
  1788→// subtractFiles returns files that are NOT in the exclude set.
  1789→func subtractFiles(files []string, exclude map[string]struct{}) []string {
  1790→	var remaining []string
  1791→	for _, f := range files {
  1792→		if _, excluded := exclude[f]; !excluded {
  1793→			remaining = append(remaining, f)
  1794→		}
  1795→	}
  1796→	return remaining
  1797→}
  1798→
  1799→// carryForwardToNewShadowBranch creates a new shadow branch at the current HEAD
  1800→// containing the remaining uncommitted files and all session metadata.
  1801→// This enables the next commit to get its own unique checkpoint.
  1802→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) carryForwardToNewShadowBranch(
  1803→	logCtx context.Context,
  1804→	repo *git.Repository,
  1805→	state *SessionState,
  1806→	remainingFiles []string,
  1807→) {
  1808→	store := checkpoint.NewGitStore(repo)
  1809→
  1810→	// Don't include metadata directory in carry-forward. The carry-forward branch
  1811→	// only needs to preserve file content for comparison - not the transcript.
  1812→	// Including the transcript would cause sessionHasNewContent to always return true
  1813→	// because CheckpointTranscriptStart is reset to 0 for carry-forward.
  1814→	result, err := store.WriteTemporary(context.Background(), checkpoint.WriteTemporaryOptions{
  1815→		SessionID:         state.SessionID,
  1816→		BaseCommit:        state.BaseCommit,
  1817→		WorktreeID:        state.WorktreeID,
  1818→		ModifiedFiles:     remainingFiles,
  1819→		MetadataDir:       "",
  1820→		MetadataDirAbs:    "",
  1821→		CommitMessage:     "carry forward: uncommitted session files",
  1822→		IsFirstCheckpoint: false,
  1823→	})
  1824→	if err != nil {
  1825→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "post-commit: carry-forward failed",
  1826→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1827→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
  1828→		)
  1829→		return
  1830→	}
  1831→	if result.Skipped {
  1832→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: carry-forward skipped (no changes)",
  1833→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1834→		)
  1835→		return
  1836→	}
  1837→
  1838→	// Update state for the carry-forward checkpoint.
  1839→	// CheckpointTranscriptStart = 0 is intentional: each checkpoint is self-contained with
  1840→	// the full transcript. This trades storage efficiency for simplicity:
  1841→	// - Pro: Each checkpoint is independently readable without needing to stitch together
  1842→	//   multiple checkpoints to understand the session history
  1843→	// - Con: For long sessions with multiple partial commits, each checkpoint includes
  1844→	//   the full transcript, which could be large
  1845→	// An alternative would be incremental checkpoints (only new content since last condensation),
  1846→	// but this would complicate checkpoint retrieval and require careful tracking of dependencies.
  1847→	state.StepCount = 1
  1848→	state.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 0
  1849→	state.LastCheckpointID = ""
  1850→	// NOTE: TurnCheckpointIDs is intentionally NOT cleared here. Those checkpoint
  1851→	// IDs from earlier in the turn still need finalization with the full transcript
  1852→	// when HandleTurnEnd runs at stop time.
  1853→
  1854→	logging.Info(logCtx, "post-commit: carried forward remaining files",
  1855→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1856→		slog.Int("remaining_files", len(remainingFiles)),
  1857→	)
  1858→}
  1859→
```

    59→// handleLifecycleSessionStart handles session start: shows banner, checks concurrent sessions,
    60→// fires state machine transition.
    61→func handleLifecycleSessionStart(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
    62→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
    63→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-start",
    64→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
    65→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
    66→		slog.String("session_ref", event.SessionRef),
    67→	)
    68→
    69→	if event.SessionID == "" {
    70→		return fmt.Errorf("no session_id in %s event", event.Type)
    71→	}
    72→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(event.SessionID); err != nil {
    73→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid %s event: %w", event.Type, err)
    74→	}
    75→
    76→	// Build informational message
    77→	message := "\n\nPowered by Entire:\n  This conversation will be linked to your next commit."
    78→
    79→	// Check for concurrent sessions and append count if any
    80→	strat := GetStrategy()
    81→	if concurrentChecker, ok := strat.(strategy.ConcurrentSessionChecker); ok {
    82→		if count, err := concurrentChecker.CountOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints(event.SessionID); err == nil && count > 0 {
    83→			message += fmt.Sprintf("\n  %d other active conversation(s) in this workspace will also be included.\n  Use 'entire status' for more information.", count)
    84→		}
    85→	}
    86→
    87→	// Output informational message
    88→	if event.ResponseMessage != "" {
    89→		message = event.ResponseMessage
    90→	}
    91→	if err := outputHookResponse(message); err != nil {
    92→		return err
    93→	}
    94→
    95→	// Fire EventSessionStart for the current session (if state exists).
    96→	if state, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(event.SessionID); loadErr != nil {
    97→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state on start: %v\n", loadErr)
    98→	} else if state != nil {
    99→		if transErr := strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStart, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
   100→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: session start transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
   101→		}
   102→		if saveErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); saveErr != nil {
   103→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state on start: %v\n", saveErr)
   104→		}
   105→	}
   106→
   107→	return nil
   108→}
   109→
   110→// handleLifecycleTurnStart handles turn start: captures pre-prompt state,
   111→// ensures strategy setup, initializes session.
   112→func handleLifecycleTurnStart(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   113→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   114→	logging.Info(logCtx, "turn-start",
   115→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   116→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   117→		slog.String("session_ref", event.SessionRef),
   118→	)
   119→
   120→	sessionID := event.SessionID
   121→	if sessionID == "" {
   122→		return fmt.Errorf("no session_id in %s event", event.Type)
   123→	}
   124→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(sessionID); err != nil {
   125→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid %s event: %w", event.Type, err)
   126→	}
   127→
   128→	// Capture pre-prompt state (including transcript position via TranscriptAnalyzer)
   129→	if err := CapturePrePromptState(ag, sessionID, event.SessionRef); err != nil {
   130→		return err
   131→	}
   132→
   133→	// Ensure strategy setup and initialize session
   134→	strat := GetStrategy()
   135→
   136→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   137→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   138→	}
   139→
   140→	if initializer, ok := strat.(strategy.SessionInitializer); ok {
   141→		agentType := ag.Type()
   142→		if err := initializer.InitializeSession(sessionID, agentType, event.SessionRef, event.Prompt); err != nil {
   143→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to initialize session state: %v\n", err)
   144→		}
   145→	}
   146→
   147→	return nil
   148→}

   165→	}

    12→
    13→// hookManager describes an external hook manager detected in a repository.
    14→type hookManager struct {
    15→	Name            string // "Husky", "Lefthook", "pre-commit", "Overcommit"
    16→	ConfigPath      string // relative path that triggered detection (e.g., ".husky/")
    17→	OverwritesHooks bool   // true if the tool will overwrite Entire's hooks on reinstall
    18→}
    19→
    20→// detectHookManagers checks the repository root for known hook manager config
    21→// files/directories. Detection is filesystem-only (os.Stat, no file reads).
    22→func detectHookManagers(repoRoot string) []hookManager {
    23→	var managers []hookManager
    24→
    25→	checks := []hookManager{
    26→		{"Husky", ".husky/", true},
    27→		{"pre-commit", ".pre-commit-config.yaml", false},
    28→		{"Overcommit", ".overcommit.yml", false},
    29→	}
    30→
    31→	// Lefthook supports {.,}lefthook{,-local}.{yml,yaml,json,toml}
    32→	for _, prefix := range []string{"", "."} {
    33→		for _, variant := range []string{"", "-local"} {
    34→			for _, ext := range []string{"yml", "yaml", "json", "toml"} {
    35→				name := prefix + "lefthook" + variant + "." + ext
    36→				checks = append(checks, hookManager{"Lefthook", name, false})
    37→			}
    38→		}
    39→	}
    40→
    41→	seen := make(map[string]bool)
    42→	for _, c := range checks {
    43→		path := filepath.Join(repoRoot, c.ConfigPath)
    44→		if _, err := os.Stat(path); err == nil {
    45→			if seen[c.Name] {
    46→				continue // e.g., lefthook.yml and .lefthook.yml both present
    47→			}
    48→			seen[c.Name] = true
    49→			managers = append(managers, c)
    50→		}
    51→	}
    52→
    53→	return managers
    54→}

    50→// getGitDirInPath returns the git directory for a repository at the given path.
    51→// It delegates to `git rev-parse --git-dir` to leverage git's own validation.
    52→func getGitDirInPath(dir string) (string, error) {
    53→	ctx := context.Background()
    54→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "rev-parse", "--git-dir")
    55→	cmd.Dir = dir
    56→	output, err := cmd.Output()
    57→	if err != nil {
    58→		return "", errors.New("not a git repository")
    59→	}
    60→
    61→	gitDir := strings.TrimSpace(string(output))
    62→
    63→	// git rev-parse --git-dir returns relative paths from the working directory,
    64→	// so we need to make it absolute if it isn't already
    65→	if !filepath.IsAbs(gitDir) {
    66→		gitDir = filepath.Join(dir, gitDir)
    67→	}
    68→
    69→	return filepath.Clean(gitDir), nil
    70→}

     1→// hook_registry.go provides hook command registration for agents.
     2→// The lifecycle dispatcher (DispatchLifecycleEvent) handles all lifecycle events.
     3→// PostTodo is the only hook that's handled directly (not via lifecycle dispatcher).
     4→package cli
     5→
     6→import (
     7→	"context"
     8→	"errors"
     9→	"fmt"
    10→	"log/slog"
    11→	"time"
    12→
    13→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    14→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode"
    15→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/copilot"
    16→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli"
    17→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging"
    18→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    19→
    20→	"github.com/spf13/cobra"
    21→)
    22→
    23→// agentHookLogCleanup stores the cleanup function for agent hook logging.
    24→// Set by PersistentPreRunE, called by PersistentPostRunE.
    25→var agentHookLogCleanup func()
    26→
    27→// currentHookAgentName stores the agent name for the currently executing hook.
    28→// Set by newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging before calling the handler.
    29→// This allows handlers to know which agent invoked the hook without guessing.
    30→var currentHookAgentName agent.AgentName
    31→
    32→// GetCurrentHookAgent returns the agent for the currently executing hook.
    33→// Returns the agent based on the hook command structure (e.g., "entire hooks claude-code ...")
    34→// rather than guessing from directory presence.
    35→// Falls back to GetAgent() if not in a hook context.
    36→func GetCurrentHookAgent() (agent.Agent, error) {
    37→	if currentHookAgentName == "" {
    38→		return nil, errors.New("not in a hook context: agent name not set")
    39→	}
    40→
    41→	ag, err := agent.Get(currentHookAgentName)
    42→	if err != nil {
    43→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("getting hook agent %q: %w", currentHookAgentName, err)
    44→	}
    45→	return ag, nil
    46→}
    47→
    48→// newAgentHooksCmd creates a hooks subcommand for an agent that implements HookHandler.
    49→// It dynamically creates subcommands for each hook the agent supports.
    50→func newAgentHooksCmd(agentName agent.AgentName, handler agent.HookHandler) *cobra.Command {
    51→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
    52→		Use:    string(agentName),
    53→		Short:  handler.Description() + " hook handlers",
    54→		Hidden: true,
    55→		PersistentPreRunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
    56→			agentHookLogCleanup = initHookLogging()
    57→			return nil
    58→		},
    59→		PersistentPostRunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
    60→			if agentHookLogCleanup != nil {
    61→				agentHookLogCleanup()
    62→			}
    63→			return nil
    64→		},
    65→	}
    66→
    67→	for _, hookName := range handler.GetHookNames() {
    68→		cmd.AddCommand(newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging(agentName, hookName))
    69→	}
    70→
    71→	return cmd
    72→}
    73→
    74→// getHookType returns the hook type based on the hook name.
    75→// Returns "subagent" for task-related hooks (pre-task, post-task, post-todo),
    76→// "tool" for tool-related hooks (before-tool, after-tool),
    77→// "agent" for all other agent hooks.
    78→func getHookType(hookName string) string {
    79→	switch hookName {
    80→	case claudecode.HookNamePreTask, claudecode.HookNamePostTask, claudecode.HookNamePostTodo, copilot.HookNameSubagentStop:
    81→		return "subagent"
    82→	case geminicli.HookNameBeforeTool, geminicli.HookNameAfterTool, copilot.HookNamePreToolUse, copilot.HookNamePostToolUse:
    83→		return "tool"
    84→	default:
    85→		return "agent"
    86→	}
    87→}
    88→
    89→// newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging creates a command for a specific hook verb with structured logging.
    90→// It uses the lifecycle dispatcher (ParseHookEvent → DispatchLifecycleEvent) as the primary path.
    91→// PostTodo is handled directly as it's Claude-specific and not part of the lifecycle dispatcher.
    92→func newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging(agentName agent.AgentName, hookName string) *cobra.Command {
    93→	return &cobra.Command{
    94→		Use:    hookName,
    95→		Hidden: true,
    96→		Short:  "Called on " + hookName,
    97→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
    98→			// Skip silently if not in a git repository - hooks shouldn't prevent the agent from working
    99→			if _, err := paths.RepoRoot(); err != nil {
   100→				return nil
   101→			}
   102→
   103→			// Skip if Entire is not enabled
   104→			enabled, err := IsEnabled()
   105→			if err == nil && !enabled {
   106→				return nil
   107→			}
   108→
   109→			start := time.Now()
   110→
   111→			// Initialize logging context with agent name
   112→			ctx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), agentName)
   113→
   114→			// Get strategy name for logging
   115→			strategyName := GetStrategy().Name()
   116→
   117→			hookType := getHookType(hookName)
   118→
   119→			logging.Debug(ctx, "hook invoked",
   120→				slog.String("hook", hookName),
   121→				slog.String("hook_type", hookType),
   122→				slog.String("strategy", strategyName),
   123→			)
   124→
   125→			// Set the current hook agent so handlers can retrieve it
   126→			currentHookAgentName = agentName
   127→			defer func() { currentHookAgentName = "" }()
   128→
   129→			// Use the lifecycle dispatcher for all hooks
   130→			var hookErr error
   131→			ag, agentErr := agent.Get(agentName)
   132→			if agentErr != nil {
   133→				return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent %q: %w", agentName, agentErr)
   134→			}
   135→
   136→			// Use cmd.InOrStdin() to support testing with cmd.SetIn()
   137→			event, parseErr := ag.ParseHookEvent(hookName, cmd.InOrStdin())
   138→			if parseErr != nil {
   139→				return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook event: %w", parseErr)
   140→			}
   141→
   142→			if event != nil {
   143→				// Lifecycle event — use the generic dispatcher
   144→				hookErr = DispatchLifecycleEvent(ag, event)
   145→			} else if agentName == agent.AgentNameClaudeCode && hookName == claudecode.HookNamePostTodo {
   146→				// PostTodo is Claude-specific: creates incremental checkpoints during subagent execution
   147→				hookErr = handleClaudeCodePostTodo()
   148→			}
   149→			// Other pass-through hooks (nil event, no special handling) are no-ops
   150→
   151→			logging.LogDuration(ctx, slog.LevelDebug, "hook completed", start,
   152→				slog.String("hook", hookName),
   153→				slog.String("hook_type", hookType),
   154→				slog.String("strategy", strategyName),
   155→				slog.Bool("success", hookErr == nil),
   156→			)
   157→
   158→			return hookErr
   159→		},
   160→	}
   161→}
   162→
```

     9→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    10→)
    11→
    12→// Compile-time interface assertions
    13→var _ agent.TranscriptAnalyzer = (*CopilotAgent)(nil)
    14→
    15→// HookNames returns the hook verbs Copilot supports.
    16→func (c *CopilotAgent) HookNames() []string {
    17→	return []string{
    18→		HookNameSessionStart,
    19→		HookNameSessionEnd,
    20→		HookNameUserPromptSubmitted,
    21→		HookNameAgentStop,
    22→		HookNameSubagentStop,
    23→		HookNamePreToolUse,
    24→		HookNamePostToolUse,
    25→		HookNameErrorOccurred,
    26→	}
    27→}
    28→
    29→// ParseHookEvent translates a Copilot hook into a normalized lifecycle Event.
    30→// Returns nil if the hook has no lifecycle significance (e.g., pass-through hooks).
    31→//
    32→// Note: In Copilot CLI, only agentStop provides sessionId and transcriptPath.
    33→// Other hooks (sessionStart, userPromptSubmitted, sessionEnd) do not include
    34→// these fields, so the resulting Event will have empty SessionID/SessionRef.
    35→func (c *CopilotAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
    36→	switch hookName {
    37→	case HookNameSessionStart:
    38→		return c.parseSessionStart(stdin)
    39→	case HookNameUserPromptSubmitted:
    40→		return c.parseTurnStart(stdin)
    41→	case HookNameAgentStop:
    42→		return c.parseTurnEnd(stdin)
    43→	case HookNameSessionEnd:
    44→		return c.parseSessionEnd(stdin)
    45→	case HookNameSubagentStop:
    46→		return c.parseSubagentEnd(stdin)
    47→	case HookNamePreToolUse, HookNamePostToolUse, HookNameErrorOccurred:
    48→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action
    49→	default:
    50→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // Unknown hooks have no lifecycle action
    51→	}
    52→}
    53→
    54→// ReadTranscript reads the raw JSONL transcript bytes for a session.
    55→func (c *CopilotAgent) ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error) {
    56→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    57→	if err != nil {
    58→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    59→	}
    60→	return data, nil
    61→}
    62→
    63→// ChunkTranscript splits a JSONL transcript into chunks.
    64→func (c *CopilotAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
    65→	chunks, err := agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
    66→	if err != nil {
    67→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to chunk JSONL: %w", err)
    68→	}
    69→	return chunks, nil
    70→}
    71→
    72→// ReassembleTranscript combines JSONL chunks back into a single transcript.
    73→func (c *CopilotAgent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {
    74→	return agent.ReassembleJSONL(chunks), nil
    75→}

    77→// ExtractPrompts extracts user prompts from the transcript starting at the given line offset.
    78→func (c *CopilotAgent) ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error) {
    79→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    80→	if err != nil {
    81→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    82→	}
    83→
    84→	return extractPromptsFromData(data, fromOffset)

    86→
    87→// ExtractSummary extracts the last assistant message as a session summary.
    88→func (c *CopilotAgent) ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error) {
    89→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    90→	if err != nil {

    92→	}

    94→}
    95→
    96→// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current line count of the JSONL transcript.
    97→func (c *CopilotAgent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {
    98→	if path == "" {
    99→		return 0, nil
   100→	}
   101→
   102→	data, err := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
   103→	if err != nil {
   104→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   105→			return 0, nil
   106→		}
   107→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   108→	}
   109→
   110→	return countLines(data), nil
   111→}
   112→
   113→// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given line offset.
   114→func (c *CopilotAgent) ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error) {
   115→	if path == "" {
   116→		return nil, 0, nil
   117→	}
   118→
   119→	data, readErr := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
   120→	if readErr != nil {
   121→		if os.IsNotExist(readErr) {
   122→			return nil, 0, nil
   123→		}
   124→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", readErr)
   125→	}
   126→
   127→	if len(data) == 0 {
   128→		return nil, 0, nil
   129→	}
   130→
   131→	totalLines := countLines(data)
   132→	extractedFiles := extractModifiedFilesFromLines(data, startOffset)
   133→
   134→	return extractedFiles, totalLines, nil
   135→}

   137→// --- Internal hook parsing functions ---
   138→
   139→// parseSessionStart handles the sessionStart hook.
   140→// Note: Copilot's sessionStart stdin does NOT include sessionId or transcriptPath.
   141→func (c *CopilotAgent) parseSessionStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   142→	_, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionStartRaw](stdin)
   143→	if err != nil {
   144→		return nil, err
   145→	}
   146→	return &agent.Event{
   147→		Type:      agent.SessionStart,
   148→		Timestamp: time.Now(),
   149→	}, nil
   150→}
   151→
   152→// parseTurnStart handles the userPromptSubmitted hook.
   153→// Note: Copilot's userPromptSubmitted stdin does NOT include sessionId or transcriptPath.
   154→func (c *CopilotAgent) parseTurnStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   155→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[userPromptRaw](stdin)
   156→	if err != nil {
   157→		return nil, err
   158→	}
   159→	return &agent.Event{
   160→		Type:      agent.TurnStart,
   161→		Prompt:    raw.Prompt,
   162→		Timestamp: time.Now(),
   163→	}, nil
   164→}
   165→
   166→// parseTurnEnd handles the agentStop hook.
   167→// This is the only hook that provides sessionId and transcriptPath.
   168→func (c *CopilotAgent) parseTurnEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   169→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[agentStopRaw](stdin)
   170→	if err != nil {
   171→		return nil, err
   172→	}
   173→	return &agent.Event{
   174→		Type:       agent.TurnEnd,
   175→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   176→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   177→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   178→	}, nil
   179→}
   180→
   181→// parseSessionEnd handles the sessionEnd hook.
   182→// Note: Copilot's sessionEnd stdin does NOT include sessionId or transcriptPath.
   183→func (c *CopilotAgent) parseSessionEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   184→	_, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionEndRaw](stdin)
   185→	if err != nil {
   186→		return nil, err
   187→	}
   188→	return &agent.Event{
   189→		Type:      agent.SessionEnd,
   190→		Timestamp: time.Now(),
   191→	}, nil
   192→}
   193→
   194→// parseSubagentEnd handles the subagentStop hook.
   195→func (c *CopilotAgent) parseSubagentEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   196→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[subagentStopRaw](stdin)
   197→	if err != nil {
   198→		return nil, err
   199→	}
   200→	return &agent.Event{
   201→		Type:       agent.SubagentEnd,
   202→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   203→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   204→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   205→	}, nil
   206→}
   207→
