```
     1→// lifecycle.go implements the generic lifecycle event dispatcher.
     2→// It routes normalized events from any agent to the appropriate framework actions.
     3→//
     4→// The dispatcher inverts the current flow from "agent handler calls framework functions"
     5→// to "framework dispatcher calls agent methods." Agents are passive data providers;
     6→// the dispatcher handles all orchestration: state transitions, strategy calls,
     7→// file change detection, metadata generation.
     8→package cli
     9→
    10→import (
    11→	"context"
    12→	"errors"
    13→	"fmt"
    14→	"log/slog"
    15→	"os"
    16→	"path/filepath"
    17→	"strings"
    18→	"time"
    19→
    20→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    21→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging"
    22→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    23→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/session"
    24→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy"
    25→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/transcript"
    26→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/validation"
    27→)
    28→
    29→// DispatchLifecycleEvent routes a normalized lifecycle event to the appropriate handler.
    30→// Returns nil if the event was handled successfully.
    31→func DispatchLifecycleEvent(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
    32→	if ag == nil {
    33→		return errors.New("agent cannot be nil")
    34→	}
    35→	if event == nil {
    36→		return errors.New("event cannot be nil")
    37→	}
    38→
    39→	switch event.Type {
    40→	case agent.SessionStart:
    41→		return handleLifecycleSessionStart(ag, event)
    42→	case agent.TurnStart:
    43→		return handleLifecycleTurnStart(ag, event)
    44→	case agent.TurnEnd:
    45→		return handleLifecycleTurnEnd(ag, event)
    46→	case agent.Compaction:
    47→		return handleLifecycleCompaction(ag, event)
    48→	case agent.SessionEnd:
    49→		return handleLifecycleSessionEnd(ag, event)
    50→	case agent.SubagentStart:
    51→		return handleLifecycleSubagentStart(ag, event)
    52→	case agent.SubagentEnd:
    53→		return handleLifecycleSubagentEnd(ag, event)
    54→	default:
    55→		return fmt.Errorf("unknown lifecycle event type: %d", event.Type)
    56→	}
    57→}
    58→
    59→// handleLifecycleSessionStart handles session start: shows banner, checks concurrent sessions,
    60→// fires state machine transition.
    61→func handleLifecycleSessionStart(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
    62→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
    63→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-start",
    64→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
    65→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
    66→		slog.String("session_ref", event.SessionRef),
    67→	)
    68→
    69→	if event.SessionID == "" {
    70→		return fmt.Errorf("no session_id in %s event", event.Type)
    71→	}
    72→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(event.SessionID); err != nil {
    73→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid %s event: %w", event.Type, err)
    74→	}
    75→
    76→	// Build informational message
    77→	message := "\n\nPowered by Entire:\n  This conversation will be linked to your next commit."
    78→
    79→	// Check for concurrent sessions and append count if any
    80→	strat := GetStrategy()
    81→	if concurrentChecker, ok := strat.(strategy.ConcurrentSessionChecker); ok {
    82→		if count, err := concurrentChecker.CountOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints(event.SessionID); err == nil && count > 0 {
    83→			message += fmt.Sprintf("\n  %d other active conversation(s) in this workspace will also be included.\n  Use 'entire status' for more information.", count)
    84→		}
    85→	}
    86→
    87→	// Output informational message
    88→	if event.ResponseMessage != "" {
    89→		message = event.ResponseMessage
    90→	}
    91→	if err := outputHookResponse(message); err != nil {
    92→		return err
    93→	}
    94→
    95→	// Fire EventSessionStart for the current session (if state exists).
    96→	if state, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(event.SessionID); loadErr != nil {
    97→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state on start: %v\n", loadErr)
    98→	} else if state != nil {
    99→		if transErr := strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStart, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
   100→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: session start transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
   101→		}
   102→		if saveErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); saveErr != nil {
   103→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state on start: %v\n", saveErr)
   104→		}
   105→	}
   106→
   107→	return nil
   108→}
   109→
   110→// handleLifecycleTurnStart handles turn start: captures pre-prompt state,
   111→// ensures strategy setup, initializes session.
   112→func handleLifecycleTurnStart(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   113→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   114→	logging.Info(logCtx, "turn-start",
   115→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   116→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   117→		slog.String("session_ref", event.SessionRef),
   118→	)
   119→
   120→	sessionID := event.SessionID
   121→	if sessionID == "" {
   122→		return fmt.Errorf("no session_id in %s event", event.Type)
   123→	}
   124→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(sessionID); err != nil {
   125→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid %s event: %w", event.Type, err)
   126→	}
   127→
   128→	// Capture pre-prompt state (including transcript position via TranscriptAnalyzer)
   129→	if err := CapturePrePromptState(ag, sessionID, event.SessionRef); err != nil {
   130→		return err
   131→	}
   132→
   133→	// Ensure strategy setup and initialize session
   134→	strat := GetStrategy()
   135→
   136→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   137→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   138→	}
   139→
   140→	if initializer, ok := strat.(strategy.SessionInitializer); ok {
   141→		agentType := ag.Type()
   142→		if err := initializer.InitializeSession(sessionID, agentType, event.SessionRef, event.Prompt); err != nil {
   143→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to initialize session state: %v\n", err)
   144→		}
   145→	}
   146→
   147→	return nil
   148→}
   149→
   150→// handleLifecycleTurnEnd handles turn end: validates transcript, extracts metadata,
   151→// detects file changes, saves step + checkpoint, transitions phase.
   152→//
   153→//nolint:maintidx // high complexity due to sequential orchestration of 8 steps (validation, extraction, file detection, filtering, token calc, step save, phase transition, cleanup) - splitting would obscure the flow
   154→func handleLifecycleTurnEnd(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   155→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   156→	logging.Info(logCtx, "turn-end",
   157→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   158→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   159→		slog.String("session_ref", event.SessionRef),
   160→	)
   161→
   162→	sessionID := event.SessionID
   163→	if sessionID == "" {
   164→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
   165→	}
   166→
   167→	transcriptRef := event.SessionRef
   168→	if transcriptRef == "" {
   169→		return errors.New("transcript file not specified")
   170→	}
   171→	if !fileExists(transcriptRef) {
   172→		return fmt.Errorf("transcript file not found: %s", transcriptRef)
   173→	}
   174→
   175→	// Early check: bail out quickly if the repo has no commits yet.
   176→	if repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository(); err == nil && strategy.IsEmptyRepository(repo) {
   177→		fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping checkpoint. Will activate after first commit.")
   178→		return NewSilentError(strategy.ErrEmptyRepository)
   179→	}
   180→
   181→	// Create session metadata directory
   182→	sessionDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(sessionID)
   183→	sessionDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(sessionDir)
   184→	if err != nil {
   185→		sessionDirAbs = sessionDir
   186→	}
   187→	if err := os.MkdirAll(sessionDirAbs, 0o750); err != nil {
   188→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session directory: %w", err)
   189→	}
   190→
   191→	// If agent implements TranscriptPreparer, wait for transcript to be ready
   192→	if preparer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptPreparer); ok {
   193→		if err := preparer.PrepareTranscript(transcriptRef); err != nil {
   194→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to prepare transcript: %v\n", err)
   195→		}
   196→	}
   197→
   198→	// Copy transcript to session directory
   199→	transcriptData, err := ag.ReadTranscript(transcriptRef)
   200→	if err != nil {
   201→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   202→	}
   203→	logFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName)
   204→	if err := os.WriteFile(logFile, transcriptData, 0o600); err != nil {
   205→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", err)
   206→	}
   207→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Copied transcript to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.TranscriptFileName)
   208→
   209→	// Load pre-prompt state (captured on TurnStart)
   210→	preState, err := LoadPrePromptState(sessionID)
   211→	if err != nil {
   212→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   213→	}
   214→
   215→	// Determine transcript offset
   216→	transcriptOffset := resolveTranscriptOffset(preState, sessionID)
   217→
   218→	// Extract metadata via agent interface (prompts, summary, modified files)
   219→	var allPrompts []string
   220→	var summary string
   221→	var modifiedFiles []string
   222→	var newTranscriptPosition int
   223→
   224→	// Compute subagents directory for agents that support subagent extraction.
   225→	// Subagent transcripts live in <transcriptDir>/<modelSessionID>/subagents/
   226→	subagentsDir := filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(transcriptRef), event.SessionID, "subagents")
   227→
   228→	if analyzer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptAnalyzer); ok {
   229→		// Extract prompts
   230→		if prompts, promptErr := analyzer.ExtractPrompts(transcriptRef, transcriptOffset); promptErr != nil {
   231→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract prompts: %v\n", promptErr)
   232→		} else {
   233→			allPrompts = prompts
   234→		}
   235→
   236→		// Extract summary
   237→		if s, sumErr := analyzer.ExtractSummary(transcriptRef); sumErr != nil {
   238→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract summary: %v\n", sumErr)
   239→		} else {
   240→			summary = s
   241→		}
   242→
   243→		// Extract modified files - prefer SubagentAwareExtractor if available to include subagent files
   244→		if subagentExtractor, subOk := ag.(agent.SubagentAwareExtractor); subOk {
   245→			if files, fileErr := subagentExtractor.ExtractAllModifiedFiles(transcriptRef, transcriptOffset, subagentsDir); fileErr != nil {
   246→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract modified files (with subagents): %v\n", fileErr)
   247→			} else {
   248→				modifiedFiles = files
   249→			}
   250→			// Get position from basic analyzer
   251→			if _, pos, posErr := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(transcriptRef, transcriptOffset); posErr == nil {
   252→				newTranscriptPosition = pos
   253→			}
   254→		} else {
   255→			// Fall back to basic extraction (main transcript only)
   256→			if files, pos, fileErr := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(transcriptRef, transcriptOffset); fileErr != nil {
   257→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract modified files: %v\n", fileErr)
   258→			} else {
   259→				modifiedFiles = files
   260→				newTranscriptPosition = pos
   261→			}
   262→		}
   263→	}
   264→
   265→	// Write prompts file
   266→	promptFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.PromptFileName)
   267→	promptContent := strings.Join(allPrompts, "\n\n---\n\n")
   268→	if err := os.WriteFile(promptFile, []byte(promptContent), 0o600); err != nil {
   269→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write prompt file: %w", err)
   270→	}
   271→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted %d prompt(s) to: %s\n", len(allPrompts), sessionDir+"/"+paths.PromptFileName)
   272→
   273→	// Write summary file
   274→	summaryFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.SummaryFileName)
   275→	if err := os.WriteFile(summaryFile, []byte(summary), 0o600); err != nil {
   276→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write summary file: %w", err)
   277→	}
   278→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted summary to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.SummaryFileName)
   279→
   280→	// Generate commit message from last prompt
   281→	lastPrompt := ""
   282→	if len(allPrompts) > 0 {
   283→		lastPrompt = allPrompts[len(allPrompts)-1]
   284→	}
   285→	commitMessage := generateCommitMessage(lastPrompt)
   286→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Using commit message: %s\n", commitMessage)
   287→
   288→	// Get repo root for path normalization
   289→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   290→	if err != nil {
   291→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   292→	}
   293→
   294→	var preUntrackedFiles []string
   295→	if preState != nil {
   296→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state: %d pre-existing untracked files\n", len(preState.UntrackedFiles))
   297→		preUntrackedFiles = preState.PreUntrackedFiles()
   298→	}
   299→
   300→	// Detect file changes via git status
   301→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preUntrackedFiles)
   302→	if err != nil {
   303→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   304→	}
   305→
   306→	// Filter and normalize all paths
   307→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   308→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   309→	if changes != nil {
   310→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   311→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   312→	}
   313→
   314→	// Filter transcript-extracted files to exclude files already committed to HEAD.
   315→	// When an agent commits files mid-turn, those files are condensed by PostCommit
   316→	// and should not be re-added to FilesTouched by SaveStep. A file is "committed"
   317→	// if it exists in HEAD with the same content as the working tree.
   318→	relModifiedFiles = filterToUncommittedFiles(relModifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   319→
   320→	// Check if there are any changes
   321→	totalChanges := len(relModifiedFiles) + len(relNewFiles) + len(relDeletedFiles)
   322→	if totalChanges == 0 {
   323→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No files were modified during this session\n")
   324→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipping commit\n")
   325→		transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   326→		if cleanupErr := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); cleanupErr != nil {
   327→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", cleanupErr)
   328→		}
   329→		return nil
   330→	}
   331→
   332→	// Log file changes
   333→	logFileChanges(relModifiedFiles, relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles)
   334→
   335→	// Create context file
   336→	contextFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.ContextFileName)
   337→	if err := createContextFile(contextFile, commitMessage, sessionID, allPrompts, summary); err != nil {
   338→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create context file: %w", err)
   339→	}
   340→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Created context file: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.ContextFileName)
   341→
   342→	// Get git author
   343→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   344→	if err != nil {
   345→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   346→	}
   347→
   348→	// Get strategy and agent type
   349→	strat := GetStrategy()
   350→	agentType := ag.Type()
   351→
   352→	// Get transcript position/identifier from pre-prompt state
   353→	var transcriptIdentifierAtStart string
   354→	var transcriptLinesAtStart int
   355→	if preState != nil {
   356→		transcriptIdentifierAtStart = preState.LastTranscriptIdentifier
   357→		transcriptLinesAtStart = preState.TranscriptOffset
   358→	}
   359→
   360→	// Calculate token usage - prefer SubagentAwareExtractor to include subagent tokens
   361→	var tokenUsage *agent.TokenUsage
   362→	if subagentExtractor, ok := ag.(agent.SubagentAwareExtractor); ok {
   363→		usage, tokenErr := subagentExtractor.CalculateTotalTokenUsage(transcriptRef, transcriptLinesAtStart, subagentsDir)
   364→		if tokenErr != nil {
   365→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to calculate token usage (with subagents): %v\n", tokenErr)
   366→		} else {
   367→			tokenUsage = usage
   368→		}
   369→	} else if calculator, ok := ag.(agent.TokenCalculator); ok {
   370→		// Fall back to basic token calculation (main transcript only)
   371→		usage, tokenErr := calculator.CalculateTokenUsage(transcriptRef, transcriptLinesAtStart)
   372→		if tokenErr != nil {
   373→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to calculate token usage: %v\n", tokenErr)
   374→		} else {
   375→			tokenUsage = usage
   376→		}
   377→	}
   378→
   379→	// Build fully-populated step context and delegate to strategy
   380→	ctx := strategy.StepContext{
   381→		SessionID:                sessionID,
   382→		ModifiedFiles:            relModifiedFiles,
   383→		NewFiles:                 relNewFiles,
   384→		DeletedFiles:             relDeletedFiles,
   385→		MetadataDir:              sessionDir,
   386→		MetadataDirAbs:           sessionDirAbs,
   387→		CommitMessage:            commitMessage,
   388→		TranscriptPath:           transcriptRef,
   389→		AuthorName:               author.Name,
   390→		AuthorEmail:              author.Email,
   391→		AgentType:                agentType,
   392→		StepTranscriptIdentifier: transcriptIdentifierAtStart,
   393→		StepTranscriptStart:      transcriptLinesAtStart,
   394→		TokenUsage:               tokenUsage,
   395→	}
   396→
   397→	if err := strat.SaveStep(ctx); err != nil {
   398→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save step: %w", err)
   399→	}
   400→
   401→	// Update session state transcript position for auto-commit strategy
   402→	if strat.Name() == strategy.StrategyNameAutoCommit && newTranscriptPosition > 0 {
   403→		updateAutoCommitTranscriptPosition(sessionID, newTranscriptPosition)
   404→	}
   405→
   406→	// Transition session phase and cleanup
   407→	transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   408→	if cleanupErr := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); cleanupErr != nil {
   409→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", cleanupErr)
   410→	}
   411→
   412→	return nil
   413→}
   414→
   415→// handleLifecycleCompaction handles context compaction: saves current progress
   416→// but stays in ACTIVE phase (unlike TurnEnd which transitions to IDLE).
   417→// Also resets the transcript offset since the transcript may be truncated.
   418→func handleLifecycleCompaction(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   419→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   420→	logging.Info(logCtx, "compaction",
   421→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   422→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   423→	)
   424→
   425→	// Fire EventCompaction to trigger ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched (stays in ACTIVE)
   426→	sessionID := event.SessionID
   427→	sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   428→	if loadErr != nil {
   429→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state for compaction: %v\n", loadErr)
   430→	}
   431→	if sessionState != nil {
   432→		if transErr := strategy.TransitionAndLog(sessionState, session.EventCompaction, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
   433→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: compaction transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
   434→		}
   435→
   436→		// Reset transcript offset since the transcript may be truncated/reorganized
   437→		sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 0
   438→
   439→		if saveErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); saveErr != nil {
   440→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to save session state after compaction: %v\n", saveErr)
   441→		}
   442→	}
   443→
   444→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Context compaction: transcript offset reset\n")
   445→	return nil
   446→}
   447→
   448→// handleLifecycleSessionEnd handles session end: marks the session as ended.
   449→func handleLifecycleSessionEnd(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   450→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   451→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-end",
   452→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   453→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   454→	)
   455→
   456→	if event.SessionID == "" {
   457→		return nil // No session to update
   458→	}
   459→
   460→	if err := markSessionEnded(event.SessionID); err != nil {
   461→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to mark session ended: %v\n", err)
   462→	}
   463→	return nil
   464→}
   465→
   466→// handleLifecycleSubagentStart handles subagent start: captures pre-task state.
   467→func handleLifecycleSubagentStart(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   468→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   469→	logging.Info(logCtx, "subagent-start",
   470→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   471→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   472→		slog.String("tool_use_id", event.ToolUseID),
   473→	)
   474→
   475→	// Log context
   476→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Subagent started\n")
   477→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Session ID: %s\n", event.SessionID)
   478→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Tool Use ID: %s\n", event.ToolUseID)
   479→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Transcript: %s\n", event.SessionRef)
   480→
   481→	// Capture pre-task state
   482→	if err := CapturePreTaskState(event.ToolUseID); err != nil {
   483→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to capture pre-task state: %w", err)
   484→	}
   485→
   486→	return nil
   487→}
   488→
   489→// handleLifecycleSubagentEnd handles subagent end: detects changes, saves task checkpoint.
   490→func handleLifecycleSubagentEnd(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   491→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   492→	logging.Info(logCtx, "subagent-end",
   493→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   494→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   495→		slog.String("tool_use_id", event.ToolUseID),
   496→		slog.String("subagent_id", event.SubagentID),
   497→	)
   498→
   499→	// Extract subagent type and description from tool input
   500→	subagentType, taskDescription := ParseSubagentTypeAndDescription(event.ToolInput)
   501→
   502→	// Determine subagent transcript path
   503→	transcriptDir := filepath.Dir(event.SessionRef)
   504→	var subagentTranscriptPath string
   505→	if event.SubagentID != "" {
   506→		subagentTranscriptPath = AgentTranscriptPath(transcriptDir, event.SubagentID)
   507→		if !fileExists(subagentTranscriptPath) {
   508→			subagentTranscriptPath = ""
   509→		}
   510→	}
   511→
   512→	// Log context
   513→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Subagent completed\n")
   514→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Session ID: %s\n", event.SessionID)
   515→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Tool Use ID: %s\n", event.ToolUseID)
   516→	if event.SubagentID != "" {
   517→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Agent ID: %s\n", event.SubagentID)
   518→	}
   519→	if subagentTranscriptPath != "" {
   520→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Subagent Transcript: %s\n", subagentTranscriptPath)
   521→	}
   522→
   523→	// Extract modified files from subagent transcript
   524→	var modifiedFiles []string
   525→	if analyzer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptAnalyzer); ok {
   526→		transcriptToScan := event.SessionRef
   527→		if subagentTranscriptPath != "" {
   528→			transcriptToScan = subagentTranscriptPath
   529→		}
   530→		if files, _, fileErr := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(transcriptToScan, 0); fileErr != nil {
   531→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract modified files from subagent: %v\n", fileErr)
   532→		} else {
   533→			modifiedFiles = files
   534→		}
   535→	}
   536→
   537→	// Load pre-task state and detect file changes
   538→	preState, err := LoadPreTaskState(event.ToolUseID)
   539→	if err != nil {
   540→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-task state: %v\n", err)
   541→	}
   542→	var preUntrackedFiles []string
   543→	if preState != nil {
   544→		preUntrackedFiles = preState.PreUntrackedFiles()
   545→	}
   546→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preUntrackedFiles)
   547→	if err != nil {
   548→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   549→	}
   550→
   551→	// Get repo root and normalize paths
   552→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   553→	if err != nil {
   554→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   555→	}
   556→
   557→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   558→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   559→	if changes != nil {
   560→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   561→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   562→	}
   563→
   564→	// If no changes, skip
   565→	if len(relModifiedFiles) == 0 && len(relNewFiles) == 0 && len(relDeletedFiles) == 0 {
   566→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] No file changes detected, skipping task checkpoint\n")
   567→		_ = CleanupPreTaskState(event.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   568→		return nil
   569→	}
   570→
   571→	// Find checkpoint UUID from main transcript (best-effort)
   572→	var checkpointUUID string
   573→	// Use the existing CLI-level checkpoint UUID finder
   574→	mainLines, _, _ := parseTranscriptForCheckpointUUID(event.SessionRef) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort
   575→	if mainLines != nil {
   576→		checkpointUUID, _ = FindCheckpointUUID(mainLines, event.ToolUseID)
   577→	}
   578→
   579→	// Get git author
   580→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   581→	if err != nil {
   582→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   583→	}
   584→
   585→	// Build task checkpoint context
   586→	strat := GetStrategy()
   587→	agentType := ag.Type()
   588→
   589→	ctx := strategy.TaskStepContext{
   590→		SessionID:              event.SessionID,
   591→		ToolUseID:              event.ToolUseID,
   592→		AgentID:                event.SubagentID,
   593→		ModifiedFiles:          relModifiedFiles,
   594→		NewFiles:               relNewFiles,
   595→		DeletedFiles:           relDeletedFiles,
   596→		TranscriptPath:         event.SessionRef,
   597→		SubagentTranscriptPath: subagentTranscriptPath,
   598→		CheckpointUUID:         checkpointUUID,
   599→		AuthorName:             author.Name,
   600→		AuthorEmail:            author.Email,
   601→		SubagentType:           subagentType,
   602→		TaskDescription:        taskDescription,
   603→		AgentType:              agentType,
   604→	}
   605→
   606→	if err := strat.SaveTaskStep(ctx); err != nil {
   607→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save task step: %w", err)
   608→	}
   609→
   610→	_ = CleanupPreTaskState(event.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   611→	return nil
   612→}
   613→
   614→// --- Helper functions ---
   615→
   616→// resolveTranscriptOffset determines the transcript offset to use for parsing.
   617→// Prefers pre-prompt state, falls back to session state.
   618→func resolveTranscriptOffset(preState *PrePromptState, sessionID string) int {
   619→	if preState != nil && preState.TranscriptOffset > 0 {
   620→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state found: parsing transcript from offset %d\n", preState.TranscriptOffset)
   621→		return preState.TranscriptOffset
   622→	}
   623→
   624→	// Fall back to session state (e.g., auto-commit strategy updates it after each save)
   625→	sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   626→	if loadErr != nil {
   627→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   628→		return 0
   629→	}
   630→	if sessionState != nil && sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart > 0 {
   631→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Session state found: parsing transcript from offset %d\n", sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   632→		return sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart
   633→	}
   634→
   635→	return 0
   636→}
   637→
   638→// updateAutoCommitTranscriptPosition updates the session state with the new transcript position
   639→// for the auto-commit strategy.
   640→func updateAutoCommitTranscriptPosition(sessionID string, newPosition int) {
   641→	sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   642→	if loadErr != nil {
   643→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   644→		return
   645→	}
   646→	if sessionState == nil {
   647→		sessionState = &strategy.SessionState{
   648→			SessionID: sessionID,
   649→		}
   650→	}
   651→	sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = newPosition
   652→	sessionState.StepCount++
   653→	if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); updateErr != nil {
   654→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", updateErr)
   655→	} else {
   656→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Updated session state: transcript position=%d, checkpoint=%d\n",
   657→			newPosition, sessionState.StepCount)
   658→	}
   659→}
   660→
   661→// createContextFile creates a context.md file for the session checkpoint.
   662→// This is a unified version that works for all agents.
   663→func createContextFile(contextFile, commitMessage, sessionID string, prompts []string, summary string) error {
   664→	var sb strings.Builder
   665→
   666→	sb.WriteString("# Session Context\n\n")
   667→	fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Session ID: %s\n", sessionID)
   668→	fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Commit Message: %s\n\n", commitMessage)
   669→
   670→	if len(prompts) > 0 {
   671→		sb.WriteString("## Prompts\n\n")
   672→		for i, p := range prompts {
   673→			fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "### Prompt %d\n\n%s\n\n", i+1, p)
   674→		}
   675→	}
   676→
   677→	if summary != "" {
   678→		sb.WriteString("## Summary\n\n")
   679→		sb.WriteString(summary)
   680→		sb.WriteString("\n")
   681→	}
   682→
   683→	if err := os.WriteFile(contextFile, []byte(sb.String()), 0o600); err != nil {
   684→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write context file: %w", err)
   685→	}
   686→	return nil
   687→}
   688→
   689→// parseTranscriptForCheckpointUUID is a thin wrapper around transcript parsing for checkpoint UUID lookup.
   690→// Returns parsed transcript lines for use with FindCheckpointUUID.
   691→func parseTranscriptForCheckpointUUID(transcriptPath string) ([]transcriptLine, int, error) {
   692→	lines, total, err := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(transcriptPath, 0)
   693→	if err != nil {
   694→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("parsing transcript for checkpoint UUID: %w", err)
   695→	}
   696→	return lines, total, nil
   697→}
   698→
   699→// transitionSessionTurnEnd transitions the session phase to IDLE and dispatches turn-end actions.
   700→func transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID string) {
   701→	turnState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   702→	if loadErr != nil {
   703→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state for turn end: %v\n", loadErr)
   704→		return
   705→	}
   706→	if turnState == nil {
   707→		return
   708→	}
   709→	if err := strategy.TransitionAndLog(turnState, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); err != nil {
   710→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: turn-end transition failed: %v\n", err)
   711→	}
   712→
   713→	// Always dispatch to strategy for turn-end handling. The strategy reads
   714→	// work items from state (e.g. TurnCheckpointIDs), not the action list.
   715→	strat := GetStrategy()
   716→	if handler, ok := strat.(strategy.TurnEndHandler); ok {
   717→		if err := handler.HandleTurnEnd(turnState); err != nil {
   718→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: turn-end action dispatch failed: %v\n", err)
   719→		}
   720→	}
   721→
   722→	if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(turnState); updateErr != nil {
   723→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session phase on turn end: %v\n", updateErr)
   724→	}
   725→}
   726→
   727→// markSessionEnded transitions the session to ENDED phase via the state machine.
   728→func markSessionEnded(sessionID string) error {
   729→	state, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   730→	if err != nil {
   731→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
   732→	}
   733→	if state == nil {
   734→		return nil // No state file, nothing to update
   735→	}
   736→
   737→	if transErr := strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStop, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
   738→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: session stop transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
   739→	}
   740→
   741→	now := time.Now()
   742→	state.EndedAt = &now
   743→
   744→	if err := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   745→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   746→	}
   747→	return nil
   748→}
   749→
   750→// logFileChanges logs the files modified, created, and deleted during a session.
   751→func logFileChanges(modified, newFiles, deleted []string) {
   752→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files modified during session (%d):\n", len(modified))
   753→	for _, file := range modified {
   754→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   755→	}
   756→	if len(newFiles) > 0 {
   757→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "New files created (%d):\n", len(newFiles))
   758→		for _, file := range newFiles {
   759→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   760→		}
   761→	}
   762→	if len(deleted) > 0 {
   763→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files deleted (%d):\n", len(deleted))
   764→		for _, file := range deleted {
   765→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   766→		}
   767→	}
   768→}
```

```
    15→// HookNames returns the hook verbs Copilot supports.
    16→func (c *CopilotAgent) HookNames() []string {
    17→	return []string{
    18→		HookNameSessionStart,
    19→		HookNameSessionEnd,
    20→		HookNameUserPromptSubmitted,
    21→		HookNameAgentStop,
    22→		HookNameSubagentStop,
    23→		HookNamePreToolUse,
    24→		HookNamePostToolUse,
    25→		HookNameErrorOccurred,
    26→	}
    27→}
    28→
    29→// ParseHookEvent translates a Copilot hook into a normalized lifecycle Event.
    30→// Returns nil if the hook has no lifecycle significance (e.g., pass-through hooks).
    31→//
    32→// Note: In Copilot CLI, only agentStop provides sessionId and transcriptPath.
    33→// Other hooks (sessionStart, userPromptSubmitted, sessionEnd) do not include
    34→// these fields, so the resulting Event will have empty SessionID/SessionRef.
    35→func (c *CopilotAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
    36→	switch hookName {
    37→	case HookNameSessionStart:
    38→		return c.parseSessionStart(stdin)
    39→	case HookNameUserPromptSubmitted:
    40→		return c.parseTurnStart(stdin)
    41→	case HookNameAgentStop:
    42→		return c.parseTurnEnd(stdin)
    43→	case HookNameSessionEnd:
    44→		return c.parseSessionEnd(stdin)
    45→	case HookNameSubagentStop:
    46→		return c.parseSubagentEnd(stdin)
    47→	case HookNamePreToolUse, HookNamePostToolUse, HookNameErrorOccurred:
    48→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action
    49→	default:
    50→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // Unknown hooks have no lifecycle action
    51→	}
    52→}
    53→
    54→// ReadTranscript reads the raw JSONL transcript bytes for a session.
    55→func (c *CopilotAgent) ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error) {
    56→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    57→	if err != nil {
    58→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    59→	}
    60→	return data, nil
    61→}

    65→	chunks, err := agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
    66→	if err != nil {

    68→	}

    70→}

    75→}

    80→	if err != nil {

    82→	}

    96→// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current line count of the JSONL transcript.
    97→func (c *CopilotAgent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {
    98→	if path == "" {
    99→		return 0, nil
   100→	}
   101→
   102→	data, err := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
   103→	if err != nil {
   104→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   105→			return 0, nil
   106→		}
   107→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   108→	}
   109→
   110→	return countLines(data), nil
   111→}
   112→
   113→// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given line offset.
   114→func (c *CopilotAgent) ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error) {
   115→	if path == "" {
   116→		return nil, 0, nil
   117→	}
   118→
   119→	data, readErr := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
   120→	if readErr != nil {
   121→		if os.IsNotExist(readErr) {
   122→			return nil, 0, nil
   123→		}
   124→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", readErr)
   125→	}
   126→
   127→	if len(data) == 0 {
   128→		return nil, 0, nil
   129→	}
   130→
   131→	totalLines := countLines(data)
   132→	extractedFiles := extractModifiedFilesFromLines(data, startOffset)
   133→
   134→	return extractedFiles, totalLines, nil
   135→}

   137→// --- Internal hook parsing functions ---
   138→
   139→// parseSessionStart handles the sessionStart hook.
   140→// Note: Copilot's sessionStart stdin does NOT include sessionId or transcriptPath.
   141→func (c *CopilotAgent) parseSessionStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   142→	_, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionStartRaw](stdin)
   143→	if err != nil {
   144→		return nil, err
   145→	}
   146→	return &agent.Event{
   147→		Type:      agent.SessionStart,
   148→		Timestamp: time.Now(),
   149→	}, nil
   150→}
   151→
   152→// parseTurnStart handles the userPromptSubmitted hook.
   153→// Note: Copilot's userPromptSubmitted stdin does NOT include sessionId or transcriptPath.
   154→func (c *CopilotAgent) parseTurnStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   155→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[userPromptRaw](stdin)
   156→	if err != nil {
   157→		return nil, err
   158→	}
   159→	return &agent.Event{
   160→		Type:      agent.TurnStart,
   161→		Prompt:    raw.Prompt,
   162→		Timestamp: time.Now(),
   163→	}, nil
   164→}

   168→func (c *CopilotAgent) parseTurnEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   169→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[agentStopRaw](stdin)
   170→	if err != nil {

   172→	}
   173→	return &agent.Event{
   174→		Type:       agent.TurnEnd,

   192→}

   194→// parseSubagentEnd handles the subagentStop hook.
   195→func (c *CopilotAgent) parseSubagentEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   196→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[subagentStopRaw](stdin)
   197→	if err != nil {
   198→		return nil, err
   199→	}
   200→	return &agent.Event{
   201→		Type:       agent.SubagentEnd,
   202→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   203→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   204→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   205→	}, nil
   206→}
   207→
```

```
   363→// extractSessionData extracts session data from the shadow branch.
   364→// filesTouched is the list of files tracked during the session (from SessionState.FilesTouched).
   365→// agentType identifies the agent (e.g., "Gemini CLI", "Claude Code") to determine transcript format.
   366→// liveTranscriptPath, when non-empty and readable, is preferred over the shadow branch copy.
   367→// This handles the case where SaveStep was skipped (no code changes) but the transcript
   368→// continued growing — the shadow branch copy would be stale.
   369→// checkpointTranscriptStart is the line offset (Claude) or message index (Gemini) where the current checkpoint began.
   370→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) extractSessionData(repo *git.Repository, shadowRef plumbing.Hash, sessionID string, filesTouched []string, agentType agent.AgentType, liveTranscriptPath string, checkpointTranscriptStart int) (*ExtractedSessionData, error) {
   371→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(shadowRef)
   372→	if err != nil {
   373→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit object: %w", err)
   374→	}
   375→
   376→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   377→	if err != nil {
   378→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit tree: %w", err)
   379→	}
   380→
   381→	data := &ExtractedSessionData{}
   382→	// sessionID is already an "entire session ID" (with date prefix)
   383→	metadataDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(sessionID)
   384→
   385→	// Extract transcript — prefer the live file when available, fall back to shadow branch.
   386→	// The shadow branch copy may be stale if the last turn ended without code changes
   387→	// (SaveStep is only called when there are file modifications).
   388→	var fullTranscript string
   389→	if liveTranscriptPath != "" {
   390→		if liveData, readErr := os.ReadFile(liveTranscriptPath); readErr == nil && len(liveData) > 0 { //nolint:gosec // path from session state
   391→			fullTranscript = string(liveData)
   392→		}
   393→	}
   394→	if fullTranscript == "" {
   395→		// Fall back to shadow branch copy
   396→		if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileName); fileErr == nil {
   397→			if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   398→				fullTranscript = content
   399→			}
   400→		} else if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileNameLegacy); fileErr == nil {
   401→			if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   402→				fullTranscript = content
   403→			}
   404→		}
   405→	}
   406→
   407→	// Process transcript based on agent type
   408→	if fullTranscript != "" {
   409→		data.Transcript = []byte(fullTranscript)
   410→		data.FullTranscriptLines = countTranscriptItems(agentType, fullTranscript)
   411→		data.Prompts = extractUserPrompts(agentType, fullTranscript)
   412→		data.Context = generateContextFromPrompts(data.Prompts)
   413→	}
   414→
   415→	// Use tracked files from session state (not all files in tree)
   416→	data.FilesTouched = filesTouched
   417→
   418→	// Calculate token usage from the extracted transcript portion
   419→	if len(data.Transcript) > 0 {
   420→		data.TokenUsage = calculateTokenUsage(agentType, data.Transcript, checkpointTranscriptStart)
   421→	}
   422→
   423→	return data, nil
   424→}
   425→
   426→// extractSessionDataFromLiveTranscript extracts session data directly from the live transcript file.
   427→// This is used for mid-session commits where no shadow branch exists yet.
   428→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) extractSessionDataFromLiveTranscript(state *SessionState) (*ExtractedSessionData, error) {
   429→	data := &ExtractedSessionData{}
   430→
   431→	// Read the live transcript
   432→	if state.TranscriptPath == "" {
   433→		return nil, errors.New("no transcript path in session state")
   434→	}
   435→
   436→	liveData, err := os.ReadFile(state.TranscriptPath)
   437→	if err != nil {
   438→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read live transcript: %w", err)
   439→	}
   440→
   441→	if len(liveData) == 0 {
   442→		return nil, errors.New("live transcript is empty")
   443→	}
   444→
   445→	fullTranscript := string(liveData)
   446→	data.Transcript = liveData
   447→	data.FullTranscriptLines = countTranscriptItems(state.AgentType, fullTranscript)
   448→	data.Prompts = extractUserPrompts(state.AgentType, fullTranscript)
   449→	data.Context = generateContextFromPrompts(data.Prompts)
   450→
   451→	// Extract files from transcript since state.FilesTouched may be empty for mid-session commits
   452→	// (no SaveStep/Stop has been called yet to populate it)
   453→	if len(state.FilesTouched) > 0 {
   454→		data.FilesTouched = state.FilesTouched
   455→	} else {
   456→		// Use the shared helper which includes subagent transcripts
   457→		data.FilesTouched = s.extractModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript(state, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   458→	}
   459→
   460→	// Calculate token usage from the extracted transcript portion
   461→	if len(data.Transcript) > 0 {
   462→		data.TokenUsage = calculateTokenUsage(state.AgentType, data.Transcript, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   463→	}
   464→
   465→	return data, nil
   466→}
   467→
   468→// countTranscriptItems counts lines (JSONL) or messages (JSON) in a transcript.
   469→// For Claude Code and JSONL-based agents, this counts lines.
```

```
     1→package strategy

    21→
    22→// SaveStep saves a checkpoint to the shadow branch.
    23→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.WriteTemporary for git operations.
    24→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) SaveStep(ctx StepContext) error {
    25→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
    26→	if err != nil {
    27→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
    28→	}
    29→
    30→	// Extract session ID from metadata dir
    31→	sessionID := filepath.Base(ctx.MetadataDir)
    32→
    33→	// Load or initialize session state
    34→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
    35→	if err != nil {
    36→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
    37→	}
    38→	// Initialize if state is nil OR BaseCommit is empty (can happen with partial state from warnings)
    39→	if state == nil || state.BaseCommit == "" {
    40→		agentType := resolveAgentType(ctx.AgentType, state)
    41→		state, err = s.initializeSession(repo, sessionID, agentType, "", "") // No transcript/prompt in fallback
    42→		if err != nil {
    43→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to initialize session: %w", err)
    44→		}
    45→	}
    46→
    47→	// Check if HEAD has changed (e.g., Claude did a rebase via tool call) and migrate if needed
    48→	if err := s.migrateAndPersistIfNeeded(repo, state); err != nil {
    49→		return err
    50→	}
    51→
    52→	// Get checkpoint store
    53→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
    54→	if err != nil {
    55→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
    56→	}
    57→
    58→	// Check if shadow branch exists to report whether we created it
    59→	shadowBranchName := checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
    60→	branchExisted := store.ShadowBranchExists(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
    61→
    62→	// Use the pending attribution calculated at prompt start (in InitializeSession)
    63→	// This was calculated BEFORE the agent made changes, so it accurately captures user edits
    64→	var promptAttr PromptAttribution
    65→	if state.PendingPromptAttribution != nil {
    66→		promptAttr = *state.PendingPromptAttribution
    67→		state.PendingPromptAttribution = nil // Clear after use
    68→	} else {
    69→		// No pending attribution (e.g., first checkpoint or session initialized without it)
    70→		promptAttr = PromptAttribution{CheckpointNumber: state.StepCount + 1}
    71→	}
    72→
    73→	// Log the prompt attribution for debugging
    74→	attrLogCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "attribution")
    75→	logging.Debug(attrLogCtx, "prompt attribution at checkpoint save",
    76→		slog.Int("checkpoint_number", promptAttr.CheckpointNumber),
    77→		slog.Int("user_added", promptAttr.UserLinesAdded),
    78→		slog.Int("user_removed", promptAttr.UserLinesRemoved),
    79→		slog.Int("agent_added", promptAttr.AgentLinesAdded),
    80→		slog.Int("agent_removed", promptAttr.AgentLinesRemoved),
    81→		slog.String("session_id", sessionID))
    82→
    83→	// Use WriteTemporary to create the checkpoint
    84→	isFirstCheckpointOfSession := state.StepCount == 0
    85→	result, err := store.WriteTemporary(context.Background(), checkpoint.WriteTemporaryOptions{
    86→		SessionID:         sessionID,
    87→		BaseCommit:        state.BaseCommit,
    88→		WorktreeID:        state.WorktreeID,
    89→		ModifiedFiles:     ctx.ModifiedFiles,
    90→		NewFiles:          ctx.NewFiles,
    91→		DeletedFiles:      ctx.DeletedFiles,
    92→		MetadataDir:       ctx.MetadataDir,
    93→		MetadataDirAbs:    ctx.MetadataDirAbs,
    94→		CommitMessage:     ctx.CommitMessage,
    95→		AuthorName:        ctx.AuthorName,
    96→		AuthorEmail:       ctx.AuthorEmail,
    97→		IsFirstCheckpoint: isFirstCheckpointOfSession,
    98→	})
    99→	if err != nil {
   100→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write temporary checkpoint: %w", err)
   101→	}
   102→
   103→	// If checkpoint was skipped due to deduplication (no changes), return early
   104→	if result.Skipped {
   105→		logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   106→		logging.Info(logCtx, "checkpoint skipped (no changes)",
   107→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   108→			slog.String("checkpoint_type", "session"),
   109→			slog.Int("checkpoint_count", state.StepCount),
   110→			slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   111→		)
   112→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipped checkpoint (no changes since last checkpoint)\n")
   113→		return nil
   114→	}
   115→
   116→	// Update session state
   117→	state.StepCount++
   118→
   119→	// Note: LastCheckpointID is intentionally NOT cleared here.
   120→	// It is set during condensation and used by handleAmendCommitMsg
```

```
    31→// hasTTY checks if /dev/tty is available for interactive prompts.
    32→// Returns false when running as an agent subprocess (no controlling terminal).
    33→//
    34→// In test environments, ENTIRE_TEST_TTY overrides the real check:
    35→//   - ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1 → simulate human (TTY available)
    36→//   - ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=0 → simulate agent (no TTY)
    37→func hasTTY() bool {
    38→	if v := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_TTY"); v != "" {
    39→		return v == "1"
    40→	}
    41→	tty, err := os.OpenFile("/dev/tty", os.O_RDWR, 0)
    42→	if err != nil {
    43→		return false
    44→	}
    45→	_ = tty.Close()
    46→	return true
    47→}
    48→
    49→// askConfirmTTY prompts the user for a yes/no confirmation via /dev/tty.
    50→// This works even when stdin is redirected (e.g., git commit -m).
    51→// Returns true for yes, false for no. If TTY is unavailable, returns the default.
    52→// If context is non-empty, it is displayed on a separate line before the prompt.
    53→func askConfirmTTY(prompt string, context string, defaultYes bool) bool {
    54→	// In test mode, don't try to interact with the real TTY — just use the default.
    55→	// ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1 simulates "a human is present" for the hasTTY() check
    56→	// but we can't actually read from the TTY in tests.
    57→	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_TTY") != "" {
    58→		return defaultYes
    59→	}
```

```
   750→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) condenseAndUpdateState(
   751→	logCtx context.Context,
   752→	repo *git.Repository,
   753→	checkpointID id.CheckpointID,
   754→	state *SessionState,
   755→	head *plumbing.Reference,
   756→	shadowBranchName string,
   757→	shadowBranchesToDelete map[string]struct{},
   758→	committedFiles map[string]struct{},
   759→) bool {
   760→	result, err := s.CondenseSession(repo, checkpointID, state, committedFiles)
   761→	if err != nil {
   762→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: condensation failed for session %s: %v\n",
   763→			state.SessionID, err)
   764→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "post-commit: condensation failed",
   765→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   766→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   767→		)
   768→		return false
   769→	}
   770→
   771→	// Track this shadow branch for cleanup
   772→	shadowBranchesToDelete[shadowBranchName] = struct{}{}
   773→
   774→	// Update session state for the new base commit
   775→	newHead := head.Hash().String()
   776→	state.BaseCommit = newHead
   777→	state.AttributionBaseCommit = newHead
   778→	state.StepCount = 0
   779→	state.CheckpointTranscriptStart = result.TotalTranscriptLines
   780→
   781→	// Clear attribution tracking — condensation already used these values
   782→	state.PromptAttributions = nil
   783→	state.PendingPromptAttribution = nil
   784→	state.FilesTouched = nil
   785→
   786→	// Save checkpoint ID so subsequent commits can reuse it (e.g., amend restores trailer)
   787→	state.LastCheckpointID = checkpointID
   788→
   789→	shortID := state.SessionID
   790→	if len(shortID) > 8 {
   791→		shortID = shortID[:8]
   792→	}
   793→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Condensed session %s: %s (%d checkpoints)\n",
   794→		shortID, result.CheckpointID, result.CheckpointsCount)
   795→
   796→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session condensed",
   797→		slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   798→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", result.CheckpointID.String()),
   799→		slog.Int("checkpoints_condensed", result.CheckpointsCount),
   800→		slog.Int("transcript_lines", result.TotalTranscriptLines),
   801→	)
   802→
   803→	return true
   804→}
   805→
   806→// updateBaseCommitIfChanged updates BaseCommit to newHead if it changed.
   807→// Only updates ACTIVE sessions. IDLE/ENDED sessions should NOT have their
   808→// BaseCommit updated, as this would cause them to be incorrectly associated
   809→// with a new shadow branch and potentially condensed on future commits.
   810→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) updateBaseCommitIfChanged(logCtx context.Context, state *SessionState, newHead string) {
   811→	// Only update ACTIVE sessions. IDLE/ENDED sessions are kept around for
   812→	// LastCheckpointID reuse and should not be advanced to HEAD.
   813→	if !state.Phase.IsActive() {
   814→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: updateBaseCommitIfChanged skipped non-active session",
   815→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   816→			slog.String("phase", string(state.Phase)),
   817→		)
   818→		return
   819→	}
   820→	if state.BaseCommit != newHead {
   821→		state.BaseCommit = newHead
   822→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: updated BaseCommit",
   823→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   824→			slog.String("new_head", truncateHash(newHead)),
   825→		)
   826→	}
   827→}
   828→
   829→// postCommitUpdateBaseCommitOnly updates BaseCommit for all sessions on the current
   830→// worktree when a commit has no Entire-Checkpoint trailer. This prevents BaseCommit
   831→// from going stale, which would cause future PrepareCommitMsg calls to skip the
   832→// session (BaseCommit != currentHeadHash filter).
   833→//
   834→// Unlike the full PostCommit flow, this does NOT fire EventGitCommit or trigger
   835→// condensation — it only keeps BaseCommit in sync with HEAD.
   836→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) postCommitUpdateBaseCommitOnly(logCtx context.Context, head *plumbing.Reference) {
   837→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
   838→	if err != nil {
   839→		return // Silent failure — hooks must be resilient
   840→	}
   841→
   842→	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath)
   843→	if err != nil || len(sessions) == 0 {
   844→		return
   845→	}
   846→
   847→	newHead := head.Hash().String()
   848→	for _, state := range sessions {
   849→		// Only update active sessions. Idle/ended sessions are kept around for
   850→		// LastCheckpointID reuse and should not be advanced to HEAD.
   851→		if !state.Phase.IsActive() {
   852→			continue
   853→		}
   854→		if state.BaseCommit != newHead {
   855→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit (no trailer): updating BaseCommit",
   856→				slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   857→				slog.String("old_base", truncateHash(state.BaseCommit)),
   858→				slog.String("new_head", truncateHash(newHead)),
   859→			)
   860→			state.BaseCommit = newHead
   861→			if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   862→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", err)
   863→			}
   864→		}
   865→	}
   866→}
   867→
   868→// truncateHash safely truncates a git hash to 7 chars for logging.
   869→func truncateHash(h string) string {
   870→	if len(h) > 7 {
   871→		return h[:7]
   872→	}
   873→	return h
   874→}

   877→// beyond what was already condensed.
   878→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) filterSessionsWithNewContent(repo *git.Repository, sessions []*SessionState) []*SessionState {
   879→	var result []*SessionState
```

```
    29→
    30→// listCheckpoints returns all checkpoints from the metadata branch.
    31→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.ListCommitted() for reading from entire/checkpoints/v1.
    32→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) listCheckpoints() ([]CheckpointInfo, error) {
    33→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
    34→	if err != nil {
    35→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
    36→	}
    37→
    38→	committed, err := store.ListCommitted(context.Background())
    39→	if err != nil {
    40→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to list committed checkpoints: %w", err)
    41→	}
    42→
    43→	// Convert from checkpoint.CommittedInfo to strategy.CheckpointInfo
    44→	result := make([]CheckpointInfo, 0, len(committed))
    45→	for _, c := range committed {
    46→		result = append(result, CheckpointInfo{
    47→			CheckpointID:     c.CheckpointID,
    48→			SessionID:        c.SessionID,
    49→			CreatedAt:        c.CreatedAt,
    50→			CheckpointsCount: c.CheckpointsCount,
    51→			FilesTouched:     c.FilesTouched,
    52→			Agent:            c.Agent,
    53→			IsTask:           c.IsTask,
    54→			ToolUseID:        c.ToolUseID,
    55→			SessionCount:     c.SessionCount,
    56→			SessionIDs:       c.SessionIDs,
    57→		})
    58→	}
    59→
    60→	return result, nil
    61→}
    62→
    63→// getCheckpointsForSession returns all checkpoints for a session ID.
    64→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getCheckpointsForSession(sessionID string) ([]CheckpointInfo, error) {
    65→	all, err := s.listCheckpoints()
    66→	if err != nil {
    67→		return nil, err
    68→	}
    69→
    70→	var result []CheckpointInfo
    71→	for _, cp := range all {
    72→		if cp.SessionID == sessionID || strings.HasPrefix(cp.SessionID, sessionID) {
    73→			result = append(result, cp)
    74→		}
    75→	}
    76→
    77→	if len(result) == 0 {
    78→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no checkpoints for session: %s", sessionID)
    79→	}
    80→
    81→	return result, nil
    82→}
    83→
    84→// getCheckpointLog returns the transcript for a specific checkpoint ID.
    85→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.ReadCommitted() for reading from entire/checkpoints/v1.
    86→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getCheckpointLog(checkpointID id.CheckpointID) ([]byte, error) {
    87→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
    88→	if err != nil {
    89→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
    90→	}
    91→
    92→	content, err := store.ReadLatestSessionContent(context.Background(), checkpointID)
    93→	if err != nil {
    94→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint: %w", err)
    95→	}
    96→	if content == nil {
    97→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("checkpoint not found: %s", checkpointID)
    98→	}
    99→	if len(content.Transcript) == 0 {
   100→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no transcript found for checkpoint: %s", checkpointID)
   101→	}
   102→
   103→	return content.Transcript, nil
   104→}
   105→
   106→// CondenseSession condenses a session's shadow branch to permanent storage.
   107→// checkpointID is the 12-hex-char value from the Entire-Checkpoint trailer.
   108→// Metadata is stored at sharded path: <checkpoint_id[:2]>/<checkpoint_id[2:]>/
   109→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.WriteCommitted for the git operations.
   110→//
```

```
   110→//
   111→// For mid-session commits (no Stop/SaveStep called yet), the shadow branch may not exist.
   112→// In this case, data is extracted from the live transcript instead.
   113→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) CondenseSession(repo *git.Repository, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, state *SessionState, committedFiles map[string]struct{}) (*CondenseResult, error) {
   114→	// Get shadow branch (may not exist for mid-session commits)
   115→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   116→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   117→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   118→	hasShadowBranch := err == nil
   119→
   120→	var sessionData *ExtractedSessionData
   121→	if hasShadowBranch {
   122→		// Extract session data from the shadow branch (with live transcript fallback).
   123→		// Use tracked files from session state instead of collecting all files from tree.
   124→		// Pass agent type to handle different transcript formats (JSONL for Claude, JSON for Gemini).
   125→		// Pass live transcript path so condensation reads the current file rather than a
   126→		// potentially stale shadow branch copy (SaveStep may have been skipped if the
   127→		// last turn had no code changes).
   128→		// Pass CheckpointTranscriptStart for accurate token calculation (line offset for Claude, message index for Gemini).
   129→		sessionData, err = s.extractSessionData(repo, ref.Hash(), state.SessionID, state.FilesTouched, state.AgentType, state.TranscriptPath, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   130→		if err != nil {
   131→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to extract session data: %w", err)
   132→		}
   133→	} else {
   134→		// No shadow branch: mid-session commit before Stop/SaveStep.
   135→		// Extract data directly from live transcript.
   136→		if state.TranscriptPath == "" {
   137→			return nil, errors.New("shadow branch not found and no live transcript available")
   138→		}
   139→		sessionData, err = s.extractSessionDataFromLiveTranscript(state)
   140→		if err != nil {
   141→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to extract session data from live transcript: %w", err)
   142→		}
   143→	}
   144→
   145→	// For 1:1 checkpoint model: filter files_touched to only include files actually
   146→	// committed in this specific commit. This ensures each checkpoint represents
   147→	// exactly the files in that commit, not all files mentioned in the transcript.
   148→	if len(committedFiles) > 0 {
   149→		if len(sessionData.FilesTouched) > 0 {
   150→			// Filter to intersection of transcript-extracted files and committed files
   151→			filtered := make([]string, 0, len(sessionData.FilesTouched))
   152→			for _, f := range sessionData.FilesTouched {
   153→				if _, ok := committedFiles[f]; ok {
   154→					filtered = append(filtered, f)
   155→				}
   156→			}
   157→			sessionData.FilesTouched = filtered
   158→		}
   159→
   160→		// If extraction failed or returned empty, use committedFiles as fallback.
   161→		// This handles mid-session commits where transcript parsing may not find files
   162→		// but we know what was committed.
   163→		if len(sessionData.FilesTouched) == 0 {
   164→			sessionData.FilesTouched = make([]string, 0, len(committedFiles))
   165→			for f := range committedFiles {
   166→				sessionData.FilesTouched = append(sessionData.FilesTouched, f)
   167→			}
   168→		}
   169→	}
   170→
   171→	// Get checkpoint store
   172→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
   173→	if err != nil {
   174→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
   175→	}
   176→
   177→	// Get author info
   178→	authorName, authorEmail := GetGitAuthorFromRepo(repo)
   179→	// Calculate attribution. When no shadow branch exists (agent committed mid-turn
   180→	// before SaveStep), pass nil ref — the function uses HEAD as the shadow tree
   181→	// since the agent's commit IS HEAD (no user edits between agent work and commit).
   182→	attribution := calculateSessionAttributions(repo, ref, sessionData, state)
   183→	// Get current branch name
   184→	branchName := GetCurrentBranchName(repo)
   185→
   186→	// Generate summary if enabled
   187→	var summary *cpkg.Summary
   188→	if settings.IsSummarizeEnabled() && len(sessionData.Transcript) > 0 {
   189→		logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "attribution")
   190→		summarizeCtx := logging.WithComponent(logCtx, "summarize")
   191→
   192→		// Scope transcript to this checkpoint's portion.
   193→		// For Claude Code (JSONL), CheckpointTranscriptStart is a line offset.
   194→		// For Gemini (JSON), CheckpointTranscriptStart is a message index.
   195→		var scopedTranscript []byte
   196→		if state.AgentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini {
   197→			scopedTranscript = geminicli.SliceFromMessage(sessionData.Transcript, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   198→		} else {
   199→			scopedTranscript = transcript.SliceFromLine(sessionData.Transcript, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   200→		}
   201→		if len(scopedTranscript) > 0 {
   202→			var err error
   203→			summary, err = summarize.GenerateFromTranscript(summarizeCtx, scopedTranscript, sessionData.FilesTouched, state.AgentType, nil)
   204→			if err != nil {
   205→				logging.Warn(summarizeCtx, "summary generation failed",
   206→					slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   207→					slog.String("error", err.Error()))
   208→				// Continue without summary - non-blocking
   209→			} else {
   210→				logging.Info(summarizeCtx, "summary generated",
   211→					slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID))
   212→			}
   213→		}
   214→	}
   215→
   216→	// Write checkpoint metadata using the checkpoint store
   217→	if err := store.WriteCommitted(context.Background(), cpkg.WriteCommittedOptions{
   218→		CheckpointID:                checkpointID,
   219→		SessionID:                   state.SessionID,
   220→		Strategy:                    StrategyNameManualCommit,
   221→		Branch:                      branchName,
   222→		Transcript:                  sessionData.Transcript,
   223→		Prompts:                     sessionData.Prompts,
   224→		Context:                     sessionData.Context,
   225→		FilesTouched:                sessionData.FilesTouched,
   226→		CheckpointsCount:            state.StepCount,
   227→		EphemeralBranch:             shadowBranchName,
   228→		AuthorName:                  authorName,
   229→		AuthorEmail:                 authorEmail,
```

```
   552→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) PostCommit() error {
   553→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   554→
   555→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   556→	if err != nil {
   557→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   558→	}
   559→
   560→	// Get HEAD commit to check for trailer
   561→	head, err := repo.Head()
   562→	if err != nil {
   563→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   564→	}
   565→
   566→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(head.Hash())
   567→	if err != nil {
   568→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   569→	}
   570→
   571→	// Check if commit has checkpoint trailer (ParseCheckpoint validates format)
   572→	checkpointID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(commit.Message)
   573→	if !found {
   574→		// No trailer — user removed it or it was never added (mid-turn commit).
   575→		// Still update BaseCommit for active sessions so future commits can match.
   576→		s.postCommitUpdateBaseCommitOnly(logCtx, head)
   577→		return nil
   578→	}
   579→
   580→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
   581→	if err != nil {
   582→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   583→	}
   584→
   585→	// Find all active sessions for this worktree
   586→	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath)
   587→	if err != nil || len(sessions) == 0 {
   588→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "post-commit: no active sessions despite trailer",
   589→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   590→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),
   591→		)
   592→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Intentional: hooks must be silent on failure
   593→	}
   594→
   595→	// Build transition context
   596→	isRebase := isGitSequenceOperation()
   597→	transitionCtx := session.TransitionContext{
   598→		IsRebaseInProgress: isRebase,
   599→	}
   600→
   601→	if isRebase {
   602→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: rebase/sequence in progress, skipping phase transitions",
   603→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   604→		)
   605→	}
   606→
   607→	// Track shadow branch names and whether they can be deleted
   608→	shadowBranchesToDelete := make(map[string]struct{})
   609→	// Track active sessions that were NOT condensed — their shadow branches must be preserved
   610→	uncondensedActiveOnBranch := make(map[string]bool)
   611→
   612→	newHead := head.Hash().String()
   613→	committedFileSet := filesChangedInCommit(commit)
   614→
   615→	for _, state := range sessions {
   616→		shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   617→
   618→		// Check for new content (needed for TransitionContext and condensation).
   619→		// Fail-open: if content check errors, assume new content exists so we
   620→		// don't silently skip data that should have been condensed.
   621→		//
   622→		// For ACTIVE sessions: the commit has a checkpoint trailer (verified above),
   623→		// meaning PrepareCommitMsg already determined this commit is session-related.
   624→		// The trailer is only added when either:
   625→		//   - No TTY (agent/subagent committing) — added unconditionally
   626→		//   - TTY (human committing) — added after content detection confirmed agent work
   627→		// In both cases, PrepareCommitMsg already validated this commit. We trust
   628→		// that decision here. Transcript-based re-validation is unreliable because
   629→		// subagent transcripts may not be available yet (subagent still running).
   630→		var hasNew bool
   631→		if state.Phase.IsActive() {
   632→			hasNew = true
   633→		} else {
   634→			var contentErr error
   635→			hasNew, contentErr = s.sessionHasNewContent(repo, state)
   636→			if contentErr != nil {
   637→				hasNew = true
   638→				logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: error checking session content, assuming new content",
   639→					slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   640→					slog.String("error", contentErr.Error()),
   641→				)
   642→			}
   643→		}
   644→		transitionCtx.HasFilesTouched = len(state.FilesTouched) > 0
   645→
   646→		// Save FilesTouched BEFORE TransitionAndLog — the handler's condensation
   647→		// clears it, but we need the original list for carry-forward computation.
   648→		// For mid-session commits (ACTIVE, no shadow branch), state.FilesTouched may be empty
   649→		// because no SaveStep/Stop has been called yet. Extract files from transcript.
   650→		filesTouchedBefore := make([]string, len(state.FilesTouched))
   651→		copy(filesTouchedBefore, state.FilesTouched)
   652→		if len(filesTouchedBefore) == 0 && state.Phase.IsActive() && state.TranscriptPath != "" {
   653→			filesTouchedBefore = s.extractFilesFromLiveTranscript(state)
   654→		}
   655→
   656→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: carry-forward prep",
   657→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   658→			slog.Bool("is_active", state.Phase.IsActive()),
   659→			slog.String("transcript_path", state.TranscriptPath),
   660→			slog.Int("files_touched_before", len(filesTouchedBefore)),
   661→			slog.Any("files", filesTouchedBefore),
   662→		)
   663→
   664→		// Run the state machine transition with handler for strategy-specific actions.
   665→		handler := &postCommitActionHandler{
   666→			s:                      s,
   667→			logCtx:                 logCtx,
   668→			repo:                   repo,
   669→			checkpointID:           checkpointID,
   670→			head:                   head,
   671→			commit:                 commit,
   672→			newHead:                newHead,
   673→			shadowBranchName:       shadowBranchName,
   674→			shadowBranchesToDelete: shadowBranchesToDelete,
   675→			committedFileSet:       committedFileSet,
   676→			hasNew:                 hasNew,
   677→		}
   678→
   679→		if err := TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventGitCommit, transitionCtx, handler); err != nil {
   680→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: post-commit action handler error: %v\n", err)
   681→		}
   682→
   683→		// Record checkpoint ID for ACTIVE sessions so HandleTurnEnd can finalize
   684→		// with full transcript. IDLE/ENDED sessions already have complete transcripts.
   685→		// NOTE: This check runs AFTER TransitionAndLog updated the phase. It relies on
   686→		// ACTIVE + GitCommit → ACTIVE (phase stays ACTIVE). If that state machine
   687→		// transition ever changed, this guard would silently stop recording IDs.
   688→		if handler.condensed && state.Phase.IsActive() {
   689→			state.TurnCheckpointIDs = append(state.TurnCheckpointIDs, checkpointID.String())
   690→		}
   691→
   692→		// Carry forward remaining uncommitted files so the next commit gets its
   693→		// own checkpoint ID. This applies to ALL phases — if a user splits their
   694→		// commit across two `git commit` invocations, each gets a 1:1 checkpoint.
   695→		// Uses content-aware comparison: if user did `git add -p` and committed
   696→		// partial changes, the file still has remaining agent changes to carry forward.
   697→		if handler.condensed {
   698→			remainingFiles := filesWithRemainingAgentChanges(repo, shadowBranchName, commit, filesTouchedBefore, committedFileSet)
   699→			state.FilesTouched = remainingFiles
   700→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: carry-forward decision (content-aware)",
   701→				slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   702→				slog.Int("files_touched_before", len(filesTouchedBefore)),
   703→				slog.Int("committed_files", len(committedFileSet)),
   704→				slog.Int("remaining_files", len(remainingFiles)),
   705→				slog.Any("remaining", remainingFiles),
   706→				slog.Any("committed_files", committedFileSet),
   707→			)
   708→			if len(remainingFiles) > 0 {
   709→				s.carryForwardToNewShadowBranch(logCtx, repo, state, remainingFiles)
   710→			}
   711→		}
   712→
   713→		// Save the updated state
   714→		if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   715→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", err)
   716→		}
   717→
   718→		// Only preserve shadow branch for active sessions that were NOT condensed.
   719→		// Condensed sessions already have their data on entire/checkpoints/v1.
   720→		if state.Phase.IsActive() && !handler.condensed {
   721→			uncondensedActiveOnBranch[shadowBranchName] = true
   722→		}
   723→	}
   724→
   725→	// Clean up shadow branches — only delete when ALL sessions on the branch are non-active
   726→	// or were condensed during this PostCommit.
   727→	for shadowBranchName := range shadowBranchesToDelete {
   728→		if uncondensedActiveOnBranch[shadowBranchName] {
   729→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: preserving shadow branch (active session exists)",
   730→				slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   731→			)
   732→			continue
   733→		}
   734→		if err := deleteShadowBranch(repo, shadowBranchName); err != nil {
   735→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: failed to clean up %s: %v\n", shadowBranchName, err)
   736→		} else {
   737→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Cleaned up shadow branch: %s\n", shadowBranchName)
   738→			logging.Info(logCtx, "shadow branch deleted",
   739→				slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   740→				slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   741→			)
   742→		}
   743→	}
   744→
   745→	return nil
   746→}
   747→

   749→// Returns true if condensation succeeded.
   750→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) condenseAndUpdateState(
   751→	logCtx context.Context,
```
