```
  1802→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) carryForwardToNewShadowBranch(
  1803→	logCtx context.Context,
  1804→	repo *git.Repository,
  1805→	state *SessionState,
  1806→	remainingFiles []string,
  1807→) {
  1808→	store := checkpoint.NewGitStore(repo)
  1809→
  1810→	// Don't include metadata directory in carry-forward. The carry-forward branch
  1811→	// only needs to preserve file content for comparison - not the transcript.
  1812→	// Including the transcript would cause sessionHasNewContent to always return true
  1813→	// because CheckpointTranscriptStart is reset to 0 for carry-forward.
  1814→	result, err := store.WriteTemporary(context.Background(), checkpoint.WriteTemporaryOptions{
  1815→		SessionID:         state.SessionID,
  1816→		BaseCommit:        state.BaseCommit,
  1817→		WorktreeID:        state.WorktreeID,
  1818→		ModifiedFiles:     remainingFiles,
  1819→		MetadataDir:       "",
  1820→		MetadataDirAbs:    "",
  1821→		CommitMessage:     "carry forward: uncommitted session files",
  1822→		IsFirstCheckpoint: false,
  1823→	})
  1824→	if err != nil {
  1825→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "post-commit: carry-forward failed",
  1826→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1827→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
  1828→		)
  1829→		return
  1830→	}
  1831→	if result.Skipped {
  1832→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: carry-forward skipped (no changes)",
  1833→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1834→		)
  1835→		return
  1836→	}
  1837→
  1838→	// Update state for the carry-forward checkpoint.
  1839→	// CheckpointTranscriptStart = 0 is intentional: each checkpoint is self-contained with
  1840→	// the full transcript. This trades storage efficiency for simplicity:
  1841→	// - Pro: Each checkpoint is independently readable without needing to stitch together
  1842→	//   multiple checkpoints to understand the session history
  1843→	// - Con: For long sessions with multiple partial commits, each checkpoint includes
  1844→	//   the full transcript, which could be large
  1845→	// An alternative would be incremental checkpoints (only new content since last condensation),
  1846→	// but this would complicate checkpoint retrieval and require careful tracking of dependencies.
  1847→	state.StepCount = 1
  1848→	state.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 0
  1849→	state.LastCheckpointID = ""
  1850→	// NOTE: TurnCheckpointIDs is intentionally NOT cleared here. Those checkpoint
  1851→	// IDs from earlier in the turn still need finalization with the full transcript
  1852→	// when HandleTurnEnd runs at stop time.
  1853→
  1854→	logging.Info(logCtx, "post-commit: carried forward remaining files",
  1855→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1856→		slog.Int("remaining_files", len(remainingFiles)),
  1857→	)
  1858→}
  1859→
```

> AGENT

Now I have a clear picture of the root cause. Let me trace the full issue:

1. **Copilot's `userPromptSubmitted` hook has no `sessionId`** → `handleLifecycleTurnStart` fails → `InitializeSession` never runs → `state.TranscriptPath` is never set
2. **`agentStop` hook has `sessionId` + `transcriptPath`** → `handleLifecycleTurnEnd` → `SaveStep` does fallback initialization with `initializeSession(repo, sessionID, agentType, "", "")` — **empty transcript path**
3. **First commit condensation**: Works because shadow branch has the transcript data from SaveStep's `WriteTemporary`
4. **Carry-forward**: Creates new shadow branch WITHOUT metadata (by design)
5. **Second commit condensation**: Fails because `state.TranscriptPath` is "" AND carry-forward branch has no transcript

Two fixes needed:

**Fix 1**: Pass `ctx.TranscriptPath` in SaveStep's fallback initialization so `state.TranscriptPath` gets set.

**Fix 2**: Return nil from copilot's `parseSessionStart` and `parseTurnStart` when no sessionId is available (instead of returning events that will fail in the lifecycle dispatcher).

```json
}
```

```
    10→func TestParseHookEvent_SessionStart(t *testing.T) {
    11→	t.Parallel()
    12→
    13→	ag := &CopilotAgent{}
    14→	// Actual Copilot sessionStart stdin format (no sessionId/transcriptPath)
    15→	input := `{"timestamp":1771465283476,"cwd":"/Users/test/project","source":"new","initialPrompt":"hello"}`

    21→	}

    23→		t.Fatal("expected event, got nil")
    24→	}
    25→	if event.Type != agent.SessionStart {
    26→		t.Errorf("expected event type %v, got %v", agent.SessionStart, event.Type)
    27→	}
    28→	// sessionStart does NOT provide sessionId or transcriptPath

    38→}
```

```
   138→
   139→// parseSessionStart handles the sessionStart hook.
   140→// Copilot's sessionStart stdin does NOT include sessionId or transcriptPath,
   141→// so we return nil (no lifecycle action). The session is initialized later
   142→// when agentStop fires with the actual sessionId.
   143→func (c *CopilotAgent) parseSessionStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   144→	_, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionStartRaw](stdin)
   145→	if err != nil {
   146→		return nil, err
   147→	}
   148→	return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action (no sessionId available)
   149→}
   150→
   151→// parseTurnStart handles the userPromptSubmitted hook.
   152→// Copilot's userPromptSubmitted stdin does NOT include sessionId or transcriptPath,
   153→// so we return nil (no lifecycle action). The lifecycle dispatcher requires sessionId
   154→// for TurnStart to capture pre-prompt state and initialize the session, but copilot
   155→// only provides this in agentStop. The prompt text is recovered from the transcript
   156→// during TurnEnd processing.
   157→func (c *CopilotAgent) parseTurnStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   158→	_, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[userPromptRaw](stdin)
   159→	if err != nil {
   160→		return nil, err
   161→	}
   162→	return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action (no sessionId available)
   163→}
   164→
   165→// parseTurnEnd handles the agentStop hook.
   166→// This is the only hook that provides sessionId and transcriptPath.
   167→func (c *CopilotAgent) parseTurnEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   168→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[agentStopRaw](stdin)
   169→	if err != nil {
   170→		return nil, err
   171→	}
   172→	return &agent.Event{
   173→		Type:       agent.TurnEnd,
   174→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   175→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   176→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   177→	}, nil
   178→}
   179→
   180→// parseSessionEnd handles the sessionEnd hook.
   181→// Copilot's sessionEnd stdin does NOT include sessionId or transcriptPath,
   182→// so we return nil (no lifecycle action). Without sessionId, the lifecycle
   183→// dispatcher cannot mark the correct session as ended.
   184→func (c *CopilotAgent) parseSessionEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   185→	_, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionEndRaw](stdin)
   186→	if err != nil {
   187→		return nil, err
   188→	}
   189→	return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action (no sessionId available)
   190→}
   191→
   192→// parseSubagentEnd handles the subagentStop hook.
   193→func (c *CopilotAgent) parseSubagentEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   194→	raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[subagentStopRaw](stdin)
   195→	if err != nil {
   196→		return nil, err
   197→	}
   198→	return &agent.Event{
   199→		Type:       agent.SubagentEnd,
   200→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   201→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   202→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   203→	}, nil
   204→}
   205→
```

> AGENT

The changes look clean. Here's a summary of what was fixed:

## Changes

### Root cause
Copilot's hook system only provides `sessionId` and `transcriptPath` in the `agentStop` hook. The `sessionStart`, `userPromptSubmitted`, and `sessionEnd` hooks lack these fields, causing lifecycle dispatcher failures and incomplete session state.

### Fix 1: `copilot/lifecycle.go` — Return nil for hooks without sessionId
- `parseSessionStart`, `parseTurnStart`, `parseSessionEnd` now return `nil` (no-op) instead of events with empty `SessionID`
- This prevents the lifecycle dispatcher from erroring out on hooks that can't do meaningful work without a session identifier
- The session is initialized later when `agentStop` fires with the actual `sessionId`

### Fix 2: `strategy/manual_commit_git.go` — Pass transcript path in fallback initialization
- `SaveStep` and `SaveTaskStep` fallback initialization now passes `ctx.TranscriptPath` instead of `""` 
- This ensures `state.TranscriptPath` is set when TurnStart didn't run (copilot's case), so second-commit condensation can read the live transcript file

### Fix 3: `e2e_test/agent_runner.go` — Ignore tools parameter (from previous session)
- `CopilotRunner.RunPromptWithTools` ignores the `tools` parameter since Copilot's `--available-tools` restricts rather than allows, and tool names differ from Claude Code's

### Updated tests: `copilot/lifecycle_test.go`
- Updated 3 individual tests and the table-driven test to expect nil events for hooks without sessionId
